金融市場投資週報                 2026 年 11 月 02 日
上週重點訊息
◼ Fed 維持利率不變，兩位委員主張升息：美國聯準會於 10 月 28 日以 10 比 2 的表決結果，
將聯邦基金利率目標區間維持在 3.75%至 4%，其中理事庫克與克里夫蘭聯準銀行總裁哈瑪
克對該決議投下反對票，並主張應升息 1 碼。Fed 在聲明中表示，經濟活動正以強勁步調擴
張，但通膨仍然偏高。在已有兩位委員主張升息的情況下，後續通膨數據將是 Fed 12 月是
否升息的重要影響因素。
◼ Alphabet 自由現金流轉負幅度超出預期：Alphabet 於 10 月 28 日公布第三季財報，自由現

金流為負 90.7 億美元，明顯低於分析師預期的負 27 億美元，同時 Alphabet 維持全年
1,950 億至 2,050 億美元的資本支出指引，顯示 AI 基礎建設投資仍持續消耗公司現金。
Alphabet 該季自由現金流明顯轉負，也引發市場對於科技巨頭資本支出的疑慮，並帶動整體
科技類股出現修正。
◼ 英特爾因 PC 需求疲弱下修第四季財測：英特爾於 10 月 29 日公布第三季財報，營收年增
21%至 165.1 億美元，高於公司原先財測；其中資料中心與 AI 部門營收年增 78%，為 15 年
來最強勁的成長，但以 PC 為主的客戶端運算部門營收則年減 5%。公司指出，由於記憶體短
缺推升 PC 售價，加上企業 IT 預算受限，使 PC 需求轉弱。
市場展望
◼ 科技股短線波動較大，中期看好現金流穩健的 AI 受惠企業：本次財報季顯示雲端營收加速成
長與未認列合約持續擴大，AI 需求仍強；但部分企業的資本支出攀升、自由現金流轉弱，加
上 Fed 可能於 12 月升息，短線科技股波動恐將延續。然而中期而言，AI 產業將由全面成長

轉向基本面分化，具技術優勢、財務結構穩健且 AI 投資已能轉化為獲利的企業，風險報酬相
對有利；對高度依賴外部融資、自由現金流持續為負的企業則宜維持審慎。
◼ 5 至 10 年期美債及投資等級公司債兼顧票息收益與利率風險：美國經濟仍具韌性，加上政府
與企業發債供給維持高檔，可能使市場利率中期仍處於較高水準，但近期公債殖利率回落，
顯示市場避險需求升溫，支撐債券買盤；整體而言，短天期債券面臨未來利率下降後的再投
資風險，長天期債券則較容易受到公債供給增加、期限溢酬及利率波動影響，5-10 年期美國
公債與投資級債券則較能兼顧息收與利率風險。

科技動能支撐股市，利率風險牽動債市配置
2026 年 9 月
全球股市 8 月截至 26 日多數上漲，其中成熟市場上漲 3.1%居冠，新興亞洲、東協股市與標普
500 也分別上漲 2.9%、2.7%及 2.6%。美股受企業財報韌性與月初就業、通膨數據降溫支撐；亞洲
則受美元偏弱、油價與長端殖利率回落帶動風險偏好改善。然而，中國股市期間下跌 0.3%，該國 7
月零售、工業生產與投資數據偏弱，加上阿里巴巴折價配股一度衝擊科技股，惟公司高層後續增持有
助緩解信心壓力。拉美股市下跌 0.8%，在主要股市中敬陪末座，主要拖累來自巴西；8 月截至 24 日，

外資淨流出巴西股市約 214 億雷亞爾，加上財政與 10 月大選的不確定性壓抑市場。印度亦小跌
0.3%，因高油價與 RBI 偏鷹訊號使公債殖利率上升。
全球債市 8 月截至 26 日則全面收紅，以新興當地債上漲 1.53%居冠，美元指數回落至 99 附近，
為其帶來匯兌加成；亞洲高收益債與全球高收益債分別上漲 1.16%、1.09%，高票息與較短存續期提
供支撐。亞洲債券、全球主權債與新興美元債表現相對落後。8 月中美國 30 年期殖利率一度升至 2007
年以來高點，壓抑長存續期債券；其後油價回落，加上 8/19 財政部宣布 9/9 起將 10 至 30 年期公債
買回上限至少倍增至每次 40 億美元，長端流動性疑慮獲緩解，30 年期殖利率回落，因而帶動債券價
格修復。
展望 9 月，市場焦點將集中於 9 月 15 日至 16 日的 FOMC 會議，以及最新經濟預測與點陣圖；
全球股市預料呈現高檔震盪格局。儘管物價漲勢稍有緩和，但 Fed 對通膨仍保持警戒；若政策立場
較市場預期鷹派，可能推升美債殖利率波動，並壓抑高估值成長股走勢。不過，AI 基礎建設與企業

科技投資趨勢延續，美國企業獲利成長亦有利科技股表現，預料中期基本面仍具支撐。台灣受 AI 與
半導體出口需求帶動，經濟成長動能強勁；日股則持續受企業治理改革與資本效率改善支撐。整體而
言，9 月股市短線震盪難免，但中期仍看好科技股，以及基本面相對強勁的台灣與日本股市。
就債市而言，美債殖利率預料仍將處於相對高檔；若就業持續降溫且通膨未再明顯升高，中期利
率上行空間可望受限。期限配置方面，短端對 Fed 政策預期較敏感，過短的存續期間亦伴隨再投資
風險；10 年以上債券則持續承受財政與期限溢酬壓力。相較之下，3 至 10 年期債券兼具較高息收、
適度存續期間與參與利率下行的空間。整體而言，目前 3 至 10 年期美國公債及投資級債的風險報酬
條件相對均衡，較具表現機會。

美國股市：AI 熱潮推升企業獲利，美股續強但留意利率風險
八月美股在高檔震盪中收高，科技股表現亮眼，除整體美股優異財報支撐指數表現外，AI 龍頭企
業如 NVIDIA 均給出強勁展望，AI 行情由晶片硬體向軟體與資安應用端擴散。指數上揚主要由輝達、
微軟、亞馬遜、帕蘭提爾、特斯拉、Meta、美光科技、賽富時、Marvell、默克藥廠等企業帶動，表
現相對弱勢的族群包括蘋果、博通、沃爾瑪、TJX、AppLovin、威騰、聯合健康、CVS、應用材料、
超微半導體等。
聯準會新任主席試圖以新的溝通模式取代傳統前瞻指引，市場解讀為基準利率有上行空間且更長

時間維持高檔。聯準會主席華許在全球央行年會(Jackson Hole)拒絕提供傳統的前瞻指引或機械式的
反應函數，強調央行過度承諾未來利率路徑會失去政策彈性，以一套「決策準則」取代指引，重點觀
察潛在通膨是否快速回到 2%、勞動市場是否偏離充分就業，以及金融條件是否有效限制總需求。由
於 7 月 FOMC 會議出現官員主張升息的情況，使短端與長端美債殖利率走勢分化，殖利率曲線進一
步陡峭化，市場正在質疑 Fed 是否具備一致且可理解的決策框架，但在華許發言後，按照這套標準，
目前政策訊號仍偏向利率在較高水準維持更長時間。由於通膨問題仍具廣度，同時信用利差偏低、股
市高檔、AI 投資與私人需求持續強勁，很難將目前金融條件視為具有限制性，故市場預期聯準會可
能不只提高升息機率，也提高了升息後維持高利率的可能性，甚至進一步提高未來降息的門檻。
第二季財報公布大致落幕，表現優於先前預期。目前標普 500 成分股已有 459 家公布 2Q26 財
報，其中 86%獲利優於市場預期，並帶動 2Q26 獲利年增達約 31.6%，較季初市場預估的 21%大幅

上修；全年獲利年增亦由 23%上修至 27%。類股獲利年增動能以能源(YoY+145%)、資訊科技
(YoY+71%)、原材料(YoY+41%)增長動能最強，醫療保健類股(YoY-7%)表現落後。資訊科技類股獲
利成長主要由半導體族群帶動，次產業中半導體獲利年增 139%、科技硬體與設備獲利年增 50.9%。
美國期中選舉前的「資料中心用電排擠效應引發的民粹反彈」持續發酵。隨著 AI 資料中心龐大
的電力需求，引發地方民意對民生與傳統產業用電資源遭擠壓的疑慮持續升溫。各州政府在選舉壓力
下，部分地方政府緊急凍結資料中心計畫，預料 11 月期中選舉後政治干擾將可降溫。
目前標普 500 指數位於歷史高檔位置，本益比已升至 23.51 倍、股價淨值比達 5.6 倍，短期評價
偏高。雖然美國科技業者目前仍居 AI 產業領先地位，但股市漲幅已高，短期仍需留意市場過熱與估
值偏高風險，建議以長線角度分批布局。

歐洲股市：景氣溫和復甦，歐洲央行升息預期與政治變數限制評價 
八月歐股延續多頭走勢，泛歐 600 指數中原物料與科技股表現相對強勢，包括思愛普、艾司摩爾
控股、Argenx、施耐德電機、力拓、嘉能可、英美資源、安聯集團、勞斯萊斯控股、西門子等表現
較佳；相較之下，部分金融與消費股表現偏弱，包括雀巢、匯豐控股、諾和諾德、英美菸草、阿斯特
捷利康、法國巴黎銀行、法國興業銀行、諾華、百威英博、萬喜等。 
歐元區八月綜合 PMI 升至 52.1，連續第 2 個月站上榮枯線，顯示第 3 季初景氣延續擴張。其中
製造業升至 52.8，創 2022 年 5 月以來最佳表現，復甦已由生產端擴散至需求端。AI 相關科技與設
備需求增加，加上國防支出擴大，帶動製造業需求回升；服務業則持平於 51.7，受惠於旅遊維持溫
和擴張。國家別來看，德國製造業 PMI 升至 54.1，創 2022 年以來最佳表現，法國製造業重返擴張
區間，但極端高溫拖累服務業活動。整體而言，八月 PMI 顯示歐元區景氣動能明顯改善，製造業加
速復甦，短期衰退風險進一步下降，然而服務業擴張力道未見加快，復甦步調呈現分化。 
部分歐洲央行官員表示，九月已有充分理由升息，利率交換市場目前反映 ECB 在 9 月會議升息
一碼的可能性。美伊戰爭推高能源成本，加上歐洲經濟表現穩健，使市場認為 ECB 仍有收緊貨幣政
策的空間。市場焦點因此由「ECB 會不會升息」轉向「升息循環還會延續多久」。中東局勢仍是最大
變數，若能源成本持續走高且逐步傳導至商品、服務價格與薪資，ECB 第四季仍可能維持鷹派立場。 
9 月歐股須留意政治局勢。若 AfD（德國另類選擇黨）於地方選舉取得大勝，雖不會直接改變目
前政府執政地位，但可能削弱改革推進速度，造成市場風險溢價上升；另外英國新任首相柏南承諾維
持既有財政規則，並受到工黨不提高所得稅、國民保險及主要稅率承諾的約束，使政府在國防、公共
服務與生活成本支出增加之際，財政操作空間相當有限，市場因此高度關注政府是否轉向其他形式的
增稅。若改革措施過度激進推高英國公債殖利率，恐將造成市場震盪。 
歐洲經濟整體呈現溫和復甦，但原油價格再次走高是最大隱憂。若能源成本居高不下，歐洲央行
與英國央行第四季仍可能維持鷹派立場，進而導致產業表現分化。銀行與金融類股可望受惠於利率走
高及淨利差改善；反之，科技、醫療保健及其他成長型類股將面臨殖利率上升所帶來的評價壓力。目
前泛歐 600 指數本益比約 16.5 倍，股價淨值比約 2.5 倍。雖然俄烏戰爭與通膨再度升溫風險仍需關
注，但在評價合理的背景下，歐股仍適合作為長線分批布局標的。

資料來源：彭博,2026/8/31

日本股市：通膨回溫拉高升息預期，半導體與內需動能推升經濟表現
八月日股受地緣政治風險與殖利率走高影響，日經 225 指數劇烈震盪。盤面上，強勢族群包括瑞
可利控股、愛德萬測試、藤倉、軟銀、松下控股、村田製作所、瑞穗金融、東京威力科創、三菱 UFJ
金融、揖斐電等；弱勢股包括東京海上控股、迅銷、大金工業、富士軟片、日本電裝、發那科、7&I、
丸紅、損保控股、KDDI 等。
8 月東京核心 CPI 升至 1.8%，雖能源補貼與食品價格降溫壓抑整體物價，但租金與醫療服務價
格明顯上升；扣除食品與能源後通膨加速至 1.4%，顯示內生性通膨壓力仍在。若就十大產品分項來

看，引導本月通膨的上漲主要來自房屋及醫療保健，其中租金價格年增幅達 1.9%，反映物價上漲持
續傳遞至房市；醫療保健價格年增幅亦自前月的 1.4%攀升至 2.8%，因政府將高額療養費制度內的自
付費用上限調高所致。根據總務省估算，日本政府對能源補貼的政策約壓抑本月通膨 0.27 個百分點，
顯示若扣除政策影響，則當前通膨約為 2.2%，仍高於日本央行所設定的物價目標。雖政府補貼政策
將持續對通膨帶來壓抑效果，使得整體通膨略低於 2%，然而搭配央行對日圓匯率過於弱勢、恐進一
步墊高通膨的擔憂，預估將促使日本央行最快於 9 月份升息一碼。
日本半導體製造裝置協會（SEAJ）公布 7 月日本製晶片設備銷售額（3 個月移動平均值）為 5,562.2
億日圓，月銷售額年增 35.4%，創下 2024 年 12 月以來最大增幅。同時，若觀察今年 1 至 7 月份累
計銷售額達 3.44 兆日圓，年增 15.7%，顯示隨著當前人工智慧墊高晶片需求，推升廠商對半導體設
備的需求。
日本 7 月外國旅客入境人數為 344.2 萬人次，年增 0.1%。本月份外國旅客人數最大宗仍是來自

南韓，達 89.5 萬人，年增 31.9%。其次則為台灣遊客的 76.4 萬人次，年增 26.4%。與之相反，來
自中國的遊客人數年減幅達 56.1%，因日中兩國關係遲遲未見改善所致。根據日本百貨店協會公布的
資料顯示，7 月百貨公司免稅銷售額年增 25.5%至 506 億日圓，連續五個月的正成長，顯示在日圓
延續弱勢的格局之下，外國旅客消費力仍有提升空間。
2026 年來日股 TOPIX 指數受惠於 AI 題材、日圓貶值、高市擴大財政刺激與強化軍事安全等政
策利多支撐，雖然日圓在政府干預與 BOJ 升息下繼續貶值空間受限，出口企業的匯兌紅利將逐步消
退，但預料日圓接下來仍以區間整理機會較高，並無大幅升值空間。目前 TOPIX 指數本益比約 17.7
倍、股價淨值比約 1.9 倍，評價仍屬合理，可利用市場震盪機會逢低分批布局。

台灣股市：工業生產創高、景氣續亮紅燈，AI 供應鏈撐起製造業擴張動能
8 月台股在 AI 龍頭財報優於預期及全球 AI 資本支出持續擴張帶動下，市場氣氛維持偏多，但受
到 Jackson Hole 會議後市場重新調整聯準會政策預期、美債殖利率攀升及美元轉強影響，指數於高
檔呈現震盪整理。資金面上，高評價 AI 族群漲多後出現輪動，市場開始重新檢視企業未來獲利成長
能否持續支撐評價水準。不過整體而言，AI 資料中心、半導體、先進封裝及伺服器供應鏈需求仍維
持強勁，有助於消化部分評價壓力，並使市場焦點重新回到企業基本面與獲利成長。

當前臺灣經濟仍處於景氣擴張階段。全球 AI 建置需求推動企業設備投資與科技資本支出維持高
檔，相關需求正逐步由晶片擴散至資料中心、網通設備、電力基礎建設及相關零組件。從國際環境觀
察，美國企業投資及私人需求維持韌性，AI 相關支出已成為支撐美國與全球科技景氣的重要力量，
也持續為台灣出口與半導體供應鏈提供結構性成長動能。
展望 9 月，市場關注焦點將由企業財報逐漸轉向總體經濟數據與聯準會政策方向。Jackson Hole
會議後，市場對於聯準會維持較高利率期間的預期升溫，短期可能提高全球金融市場波動。然而，從
企業基本面觀察，NVIDIA 最新展望顯示 AI 需求仍具高度能見度，全球雲端業者與大型企業的 AI 基
礎建設支出並未出現明顯放緩跡象。在此背景下，只要半導體、伺服器及先進封裝產業維持強勁接單
動能，台灣電子產業景氣仍有機會延續高檔表現。
投資策略方面，仍可維持對 AI 供應鏈的正向看法，但須更重視獲利成長與評價合理性。除了先
進製程及先進封裝外，AI 伺服器組裝、散熱、電源管理、高速傳輸、網通設備及記憶體等受惠 AI 基

建擴張之族群，仍具備中長期成長機會。隨著 AI 投資逐步由 GPU 擴散至記憶體、網路交換器、光通
訊及系統整合等領域，受惠範圍亦由少數龍頭供應商進一步擴展至整體供應鏈，有助於支撐相關企業
未來營收與獲利成長動能。另一方面，9 月需留意美國 CPI 數據、聯準會利率決策及美債殖利率變化
對高評價科技股的影響；若市場因政策預期、資金輪動或短期獲利了結而出現回檔，較可能屬於多頭
趨勢中的震盪整理，而非基本面反轉。在企業獲利持續成長、AI 資本支出維持高檔及全球科技景氣
延續復甦的背景下，建議布局方向可由單純題材面轉向具備技術門檻、訂單能見度及現金流品質較佳
的個股，以提升投資組合在景氣循環與市場波動中的穩健度。

中國股市：硬科技 IPO 熱潮延燒，A 股中報揭露「增收不增利」普遍現象
8 月中國經濟延續「總量承壓、科技產業相對強勢」的分化格局。儘管房地產調整與內需復甦力
道仍有限，但高端製造、人工智慧及數位經濟仍是政策資源與市場資金聚焦方向。在傳統產業景氣復
甦較為緩慢的情況下，科技產業逐漸成為支撐經濟成長的重要動能，顯示中國經濟正持續朝新舊動能
轉換與產業升級方向發展。
科技產業方面，人形機器人已成為近期最受市場關注的新興產業之一。根據中國國家市場監管總
局統計，2026 年上半年人形機器人領域新設企業數量達 11.6 萬家，較 2025 年同期的 7.2 萬家大幅

成長 61.1%，反映資本投入、技術研發與產業參與程度快速提升。隨著人工智慧、大模型及感測技術
進步，人形機器人正逐步由研發驗證邁向商業化應用階段，產業發展重點也由概念題材轉向量產能
力、產品可靠度及終端需求驗證。
資本市場表現雖顯示科技板塊仍是 A 股資金主要聚焦方向，但企業基本面改善幅度與市場期待之
間仍存在落差。從滬深 300 成分股來看，科技類企業近年營收維持增長，但獲利表現並未同步大幅
提升，反映部分企業仍面臨研發投入增加、產能擴張及市場競爭加劇等壓力。AI、半導體及高端製造
雖持續獲得政策支持與資本關注，但不少企業的估值已提前反映未來成長預期，實際獲利貢獻仍有待
進一步驗證。市場關注焦點也逐漸由政策題材與自主替代概念，轉向訂單能見度、現金流與獲利能力
等基本面指標；若後續營運表現未能跟上市場預期，不排除科技板塊仍面臨評價修正壓力。
展望 9 月，政策方向預計仍以穩增長與培育新質生產力為主軸，人工智慧、人形機器人、高端製
造及半導體等領域仍將是支持重點。在整體經濟復甦力道尚未全面擴散的背景下，市場表現預期仍將

呈現結構性分化，資金持續聚焦具成長潛力與技術門檻的科技產業。投資策略上，可留意人形機器人
產業鏈、工業自動化、AI 應用、半導體設備及高端製造等受惠產業升級趨勢之族群；惟部分熱門題
材評價已反映較高成長預期，後續仍須觀察訂單落地、量產進度及獲利改善情況，避免因短期市場情
緒過熱而提高投資風險。

科技類股：AI 基礎建設持續擴張，算力、光通訊與平台生態同步受惠
全球科技產業的核心成長動能仍來自 AI 基礎建設擴張，但市場焦點已逐步由單純 GPU 採購轉向
整體資料中心架構升級。隨 Google 擴大投入光通訊與高速網路技術，AI 資料中心正朝向更高頻寬、
更低延遲及更高能效方向發展。市場預估 800G 至 1.6T 光通訊滲透率將持續提升，推動光模組、CPO、
交換器及相關供應鏈需求成長，也反映大型雲端服務供應商仍持續強化 AI 基礎建設布局。在此趨勢
下，AI 投資已由單一晶片擴展至網路、儲存、記憶體及先進封裝等完整生態系，帶動相關供應鏈重

新評價。
企業獲利方面，NVIDIA 最新財報再次驗證 AI 投資循環仍具延續性。公司第二季營收與資料中心
業務均大幅優於預期，資料中心網路業務亦維持高速成長，顯示大型企業、雲端服務業者及 AI 模型
開發商對算力需求仍然強勁。同時，管理層對第三季營收及新一代 Vera Rubin 平台維持正向展望，
反映 AI 基礎建設建置並未出現明顯放緩跡象。不過，市場對 AI 產業的評價標準已逐漸由需求成長轉
向獲利品質與投資效益，未來資本支出、折舊攤提與現金流變化仍將是投資人關注重點。
從產業鏈角度來看，AI 需求正持續向外擴散。除了 GPU 之外，高速光通訊、網路交換器、高頻
寬記憶體(HBM)、CPU 及資料中心電力架構均成為下一階段重要受惠領域。另一方面，NVIDIA 近期
宣布結合金融機構與雲端服務資源打造大型 AI 基礎設施平台，顯示產業發展正由硬體銷售進一步延
伸至算力服務與平台營運模式。此舉有利強化 AI 產業生態系並提高資本取得效率，但也反映未來競
爭重點將由單純硬體性能逐步轉向平台整合能力、商業模式及資金運用效率。隨著更多企業投入 AI

建設，市場也開始關注高額資本支出是否能持續帶來相應回報，產業獲利表現仍可能出現明顯分化。
展望後續，AI 仍是科技產業最具確定性的投資主軸，但市場關注焦點正由「建置多少算力」逐步
轉向「創造多少效益」。NVIDIA 新平台推廣進度、Vera Rubin 產品放量、高速光通訊滲透率提升
及 HBM 供給變化，將是觀察下一階段 AI 投資循環的重要指標。雖然大型雲端業者資本支出尚未出
現明顯下修，支撐整體供應鏈需求維持高檔，但隨著資料中心建置成本、能源支出與折舊費用持續增
加，企業獲利能力與現金流表現的重要性亦將提升。未來較具競爭優勢的企業，除了掌握核心技術外，
更需具備規模化商業模式及資本效率，才能在 AI 產業由高速成長邁向成熟發展的過程中持續創造價
值。

礦業類股：關稅預期有利銅價支撐，戰略庫存需求是潛在利多
8 月以來礦業類股走勢強勁，倫敦金屬交易所銅價也再創歷史新高，這波上漲的主要動能，仍是
來自於我們先前一再提到的美國潛在銅關稅，改變了全球銅庫存的流向。由於市場擔心美國未來可能
對精煉銅加徵關稅，交易商提前將銅運往美國，希望在關稅實施前完成進口，導致其他地區能夠取得
的現貨供應相應減少，在供給減少下持續為銅價帶來支撐。
截至 8 月底，美國現行關稅主要涵蓋銅棒、銅線、銅板及銅管等半成品與衍生產品，精煉銅尚未
正式納入課稅範圍，但市場普遍預估美國政府將有可能在 2027 年開始實施精煉銅關稅，帶動交易商

提前採取備貨行動。路透社報導指出，美國 2026 年上半年進口約 88.5 萬噸精煉銅，年增約 3%，紐
約商品交易所銅庫存也攀升至約 67.5 萬噸的歷史高點。若將時間拉長至一年觀察，全球三大交易所
合計增加約 49.58 萬噸庫存，其中約 81%集中於紐約商品交易所，顯示全球可見銅庫存雖然上升，
但新增庫存大多被鎖在美國，非美市場銅庫存相對處於較為緊張的狀態。
除了關稅帶動的庫存失衡外，銅的實際應用需求也具有基本面支撐。彭博行業研究預估，2026
年全球銅需求將成長 2.4%至 2.7%，增速雖較 2025 年放緩，但電動車、再生能源、資料中心及電網
升級仍將帶動用銅需求上升。其中雖然中國銅需求成長預估也將放緩，但是中國國家電網已規劃 2026
至 2030 年投資 4 兆元人民幣，較前五年增加 44%，以支應人工智慧用電需求。
另一方面，隨著區域銅庫存出現短缺及 AI 帶動用銅需求上升，若政府與企業在未來計畫提高安
全庫存，將可能成為市場尚未充分反映的中長期上漲基礎。花旗研究估算，目前全球精煉銅總庫存約

為 300 萬噸，僅相當於 1.3 個月的全球消費量。若各國政府及大型供應鏈基於國防、能源安全、人
工智慧基礎建設或地緣政治風險，將安全庫存提高至兩個月，市場約需要額外累積 170 萬噸銅庫存；
若提高至三個月，額外需求更將擴大至約 400 萬噸。由於新礦山從開發到投產需要多年，供給無法
在短期內迅速增加，市場只能透過銅價上漲，促使更多廢銅進入市場，同時迫使部分使用者節約用銅
或尋找替代材料。因此，建立庫存的規模愈大、完成時間愈短，銅價需要調整的漲幅也將愈顯著。
展望後市，儘管美國精煉銅關稅目前尚未正式實施，但市場預期帶來的提前備貨潮仍有利短線銅
價支撐，同時全球用銅需求仍維持成長，新礦山供給又難以快速實現，加上政府及企業可能逐步提高
安全庫存，使銅市場的中長期供需結構仍具有支撐，故對具銅價曝險的礦業類股中長期後市仍可正向
看待，然而仍需留意若未來銅關稅未如市場預期實施，當前囤積於美國市場的銅庫存將可能回歸到全
球市場當中，為銅價帶來短線波動。

### Page 9

任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本
身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元大銀行所有，非經本行同意，
不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕不另行通知。
能源類股：荷莫茲海峽持續受阻，惟實際供應缺口與庫存降幅低於市場預期
8 月以來，美伊衝突持續反覆升降溫，雙方時而釋出談判訊號，時而再度發生軍事衝突，短期內
仍未出現實質進展，荷莫茲海峽通行亦持續受限。然而，海峽受阻並不代表原有運量全數消失，部分
原油仍可經由海運與陸上替代管線出口，同時中國石油需求下降及各國政府釋放戰略庫存亦抵銷部分
中斷影響。
彭博行業研究指出，儘管當前荷莫茲海峽受到干擾，但實際並未呈現完全封鎖的狀態，目前仍有
零星油輪持續通行。彭博指出，由於部分油輪會關閉公開定位訊號，並在通過海峽後於阿曼外海進行
轉運，因此公開船舶資料可能會漏算部分運量。彭博綜合目前庫存變化與市場實際供需後，估計目前

每日仍有約 750 萬桶原油通過荷莫茲海峽，意味僅依據可觀察船舶數量推算中東石油供應中斷量，
可能高估了市場實際缺口。
除了每日仍有約 750 萬桶原油經過海峽運輸外，彭博估計目前另有每日約 500 萬桶石油改走陸
上管線；需求端則因中國每日減少約 400 萬桶進口而下降，其他地區減少用油與各國政府釋放戰略
庫存再合計填補每日約 200 萬桶石油缺口。經過上述調整後，原本每日約 2,000 萬桶經荷莫茲海峽
通行的運量中，實際供應缺口僅剩每日約 150 萬桶，這也解釋了當前油價漲幅為何未完全反映表面
的產量損失。
全球石油庫存變化的數據亦呼應了實際缺口低於市場普遍預期的論點。花旗報告指出，2026 年 2
月至 8 月全球實際觀察到的石油供需缺口平均約為每日 290 萬桶，明顯低於其原先預估的每日 700
萬至 800 萬桶。截至 2026 年 8 月，全球石油庫存仍可覆蓋 99 天需求，亦仍處於 2022 年至 2025
年的 95 至 101 天區間。花旗估計，若目前庫存下降速度延續，全球庫存要到 2029 年第一季才會降

至接近第二次石油危機緊張程度的 70 天水位，這顯示荷莫茲海峽若持續受阻，雖油價上漲壓力仍會
逐步累積，但實際庫存下降速度並未如市場普遍預期的嚴峻。
展望後市，除荷莫茲海峽中斷造成的供應缺口與庫存降幅低於市場預期外，國際能源署也預估
2027 年全球石油需求將增加每日 240 萬桶，供應則可能年增每日 830 萬桶，意味若中東產量與海峽
通行逐步恢復，供應回升速度將明顯快於需求，並帶動庫存回補，不利油價上漲趨勢，然而考量短線
地緣風險仍有利能源類股形成支撐，在美伊達成長期停火協議前，應中性看待能源類股後市表現。

任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本
身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元大銀行所有，非經本行同意，
不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕不另行通知。
黃金類股：央行買盤支撐中長期價格，惟升息預期帶動短線波動升高
8 月金價走勢先揚後抑。月初公布的美國就業數據偏弱，加上中國及全球黃金 ETF 買盤回升，使
市場一度降低近期升息預期，金價因而突破 7 月的整理區間。到了 8 月中旬，美國財政部宣布擴大
長天期公債回購，帶動長端殖利率與美元回落，進一步放大黃金漲勢；不過，月底公布的通膨數據仍
顯示物價壓力具有黏性，聯準會主席華許在傑克森霍爾會議的態度也偏向鷹派，使市場重新提高升息
預期，金價隨即自高檔回落。然而即使月底出現修正，金價在 8 月仍累積明顯漲幅。
支撐金價中長期走勢的核心力量，仍然來自世界各國央行為分散外匯儲備，持續增持黃金。世界

黃金協會指出，2026 年第 2 季全球央行淨購金量為 289 噸，累計 2026 年上半年約 345 噸。雖然上
半年央行購金量受到土耳其、俄羅斯等國調節部位影響，降至 2022 年以來同期低點，但第 2 季購金
量已較第 1 季明顯回升，而且買盤仍分布於波蘭、中國、烏茲別克及哈薩克等多個市場，顯示各國央
行增持黃金的趨勢仍未出現轉變，且可觀察到各國央行對於逢低承接黃金的意願。
在央行需求之外，中國投資資金是本輪金價突破的重要邊際買盤。2026 年上半年中國黃金淨進
口量達 764 噸，較 2025 年同期增加 138%，其規模明顯高於中國人民銀行公布的增持量，顯示民間
與機構投資人也在提高黃金配置。當中國資金推動金價突破整理區間後，美國商品期貨交易委員會的
資料也顯示，管理基金黃金淨多單在 8 月持續增加，使原本集中於亞洲的漲勢進一步獲得美國期貨資
金支持。
然而，投資資金參與程度提高，也使金價對利率與美元變化更加敏感。美國財政部 8 月 19 日宣
布，將至少加倍 10 年至 30 年期公債的流動性支持回購規模，市場隨即下調長端殖利率，黃金也在

持有成本下降後快速上漲。不過，月底公布的美國 7 月通膨數據仍高，加上華許在傑克森霍爾會議維
持偏鷹立場，再次使市場重新認為聯準會仍可能透過升息抑制通膨，美元與殖利率回升後，部分短線
資金便出現獲利了結賣壓。
展望後市，各國央行增持黃金儲備仍支撐黃金的中長期多頭結構，而美國財政與債務可持續性疑
慮也將持續提高黃金的分散風險價值。不過，8 月底的回檔顯示，當金價漲勢由 ETF 及期貨資金放大
後，實質利率與美元只要轉為上升，短線市場部位便可能迅速調整，故在美國後續利率政策仍具有不
確定性的狀況下，對於黃金類股後市應中性看待。

固定收益：長端風險仍存，3~10 年期公債、投資級債更具表現機會
8 月以來，美國公債殖利率維持高檔震盪，若以 7 月底與 8/24 比較，各年期債券殖利率近乎平
行小幅下行。8 月初公布的 7 月非農就業意外減少 2.3 萬人，顯示勞動市場動能轉弱；7 月 CPI 年增
率由 3.5%降至 3.4%，核心 CPI 年增率亦由 2.6%降至 2.5%，一度緩解市場對 Fed 緊縮疑慮。惟 7
月 PPI 與 PCE 數據表現，限制利率進一步下行空間。
8 月中旬之後，市場焦點由政策利率逐步轉向能源價格、財政供給與期限溢酬。美伊局勢反覆推

升油價，加上美國聯邦債務 8 月首度突破 40 兆美元，2 年與 30 年期利差由 7 月底 99 個基點擴大至
8/18 的 109 個基點，30 年期殖利率亦升至 5.28%。美國財政部 8/19 宣布，自 9/9 起將部分 10～
30 年期公債單次流動性回購上限由 20 億美元提高至至少 40 億美元，帶動 30 年期殖利率由 5.28%
快速回落至 5.19%。截至 8/24，2 年與 30 年利差亦回到 99 個基點，月中陡峭化大致回吐。此一走
勢顯示，回購措施雖有助改善長券流動性，但財政赤字、公債供給及期限溢酬等結構性壓力仍未消失。
全球主要成熟國家的長端債券亦面臨類似壓力。德國 30 年期公債殖利率近期升至約 3.79%，創
2011 年以來最高；日本 10 年期公債殖利率 8/18 盤中一度觸及 2.945%，亦為 1996 年 9 月以來高
位，30 年期亦一度升至 4.115%。成熟市場長債同步承壓，反映財政擴張、通膨風險與貨幣政策正常
化正共同推升長端期限溢酬。
不過，信用市場整體仍相對穩定，截至 8/24，聖路易聯準銀行的 ICE 美國銀行美國投資級公司

債 OAS 約 81 個基點、有效殖利率約 5.4%，但信用利差仍處歷史偏低區間，緩衝並不寬裕。AI 基礎
建設融資的供給壓力亦已開始反映在定價，Reuters 8/21 指出，2026 年至 8 月上旬主要 AI 雲端業
者發債規模已約 2,200 億美元，科技類投資級債利差升至 89 個基點，較整體 IG 市場高出約 9 個基
點；近期 Alphabet 新券亦需提供約 10～15 個基點讓利，顯示在企業基本面仍穩健之際，大量新券
供給已成為影響信用評價的重要因素。
展望 9 月，美債殖利率預料仍將處於相對高檔，若就業持續降溫且通膨未再明顯升高，中期利率
上行空間可望受限。期限配置方面，短端對 Fed 政策預期較敏感，過短存續期間亦有再投資風險；
10 年以上債券則持續承受財政與期限溢酬壓力。值得注意的是，截至 8/27，10 年與 30 年期殖利率
利差為 52 個基點，2 年至 30 年總利差 96 個基點，顯示 2 年至 30 年期殖利率曲線陡峭幅度中，逾
半來自 10~30 年區間，反映超長端相對 10 年期仍有明顯期限溢酬。相較之下，3～10 年期兼具較

高息收、適度存續期間與利率下行參與空間。整體而言，目前 3～10 年期美國公債及投資級債的風
險報酬條件相對均衡，料具表現機會。

漢唐(2404 TT) Buy

3Q26 獲利優於預期，客戶建廠需求暢旺，給 Buy 的投資建
議。 
投資建議： 3Q26 獲利優於預期，加上在手訂單金額約 1,939 億
元，有助於未來漢唐營運，給 Buy 的投資建議。 
漢唐 3Q26 獲利優於預期：漢唐 3Q26 營收 275.06 億元，
QoQ+5.47%；稅後純益 45.69 億元，QoQ+21.43%；稅 後 EPS 23.97
元。3Q26 稅後純益優於原先預估的 39.27 億元，主因毛利率優
於預期。 
漢唐目前在手訂單金額約 1,939 億元：漢唐 01~05/2026 年接單
合約金額約 674 億元，截至 05/2026 年已簽約未認列之金額(在
手訂單)約 1,939 億元。在手訂單約 24~36 個月可以認列大部分。
目前在手訂單金額的施工地區，有 2/3 在台灣，1/3 在海外。 
24~36 個月認列大部分的在手訂單可能是保守估計，2H26 在手
訂單金額可能還會再增加。 
漢唐對營運樂觀：漢唐表示目前建廠需求遠大於供給，因目前
人力短缺，尤其是在台灣，暫時沒辦法去接其他大型客戶的訂
單。由於 AI 與相關需求，現在客戶建廠的需求會延續到 2029
年，漢唐對整體產業的展望表示樂觀。

個股報告 
                                        2026 年 11 月 13 日

2 群益投顧 
  ESG：  
永續環境是漢唐經營理念之綠色承諾，因應全球環保節能的需求，我們透過
工程設計、系統整合提升工程效率，減少工程過程中能耗。漢唐集成響應政
府綠色採購辦法，我們從原物料取得、工程施作、運輸配送、完工至客戶使
用階段，優先要求廠商提供低污染、可回收、再利用之環保產品，以達到廢
棄物減量之目標。 
漢唐集成根著台灣，從初創之施工安裝小公司成長為專業工程集成公司，有
賴於各地政通人和，因此，漢唐集成本著感恩的心回饋在地，不斷捐助弱勢
及支持培育英才，期望肩擔起企業之「社會責任」，讓台灣變得更美好。漢
唐股東會由全體股東組成，對公司重大事項進行決策，定期聽取董事會報
告，為公司最高決策機關；董事會為最高治理機關，董事會成員皆恪盡善良
管理人之注意義務，擘劃公司的經營政策與檢視財務績效，並確保公司營運
遵守各種法令；為完善公司治理運作、強化公司競爭力，董事會設置審計委
員會與薪資報酬委員會來健全董事會運作；董事會之下亦設有獨立之稽核
室，定期執行稽核業務並向審計委員會與董事會呈報稽核結果。 
公司簡介： 
漢唐集成成立於 1982 年，為一以「全面性整合服務」方式，專業提供高科
技產業建廠之系統集成服務公司；主要業務範圍包括高科技廠房各系統，包
括電腦、控制、無塵室、機電、電信系統之施作與整合服務。 
漢唐目前在手訂單金額約 1,939 億元： 
漢唐 01~05/2026 年接單合約金額約 674 億元，截至 05/2026 年已簽約未認
列之金額(在手訂單)約 1,939 億元。在手訂單約 24~36 個月可以認列大部
分。目前在手訂單金額的施工地區，有 2/3 在台灣，1/3 在海外。 
24~36 個月認列大部分的在手訂單可能是保守估計，2H26 在手訂單金額可
能還會再增加。 
漢唐未來還有先進封裝廠的訂單： 
大客戶於美國建廠的第三期工程的訂單目前漢唐已經有接到一部份，還沒
有認列到手訂單金額中，客戶的先進封裝廠的訂單還沒發包。第二期工程進
度希望在 2026 年認列約 85%，第三期工程的認列狀況要看客戶，未來還有
先進封裝廠的訂單。 
美國營收已超過台灣： 
漢唐沒有透露前兩大客戶的營收占比。2025 年營收占比_地區別：台灣 65%、
美國 25%、其他 9%。 1Q26 台灣 40%、美國 56%、其他 4%。美系記憶體廠
在台灣蓋無塵室，漢唐還是主要的供應商。 
漢唐對營運樂觀： 
漢唐表示目前建廠需求遠大於供給，因目前人力短缺，尤其是在台灣，暫時
沒辦法去接其他大型客戶的訂單。由於 AI 與相關需求，現在客戶建廠的需
求會延續到 2029 年，漢唐對整體產業的展望表示樂觀。

其他補充： 
漢唐的費用比較固定，管理費用包含員工酬勞。漢唐追求與客戶長期合作，
如果是原物料上漲導致成本增加，客戶可以接受漲價。 
目前漢唐在全球的員工人數約 1,700 人，美國、台灣都會再增加人力，之後
也是要看下包廠商，看是否能擴大供應鏈。

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】 
  評等 定義 
  強力買進(Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間≥ 35%  
  買進(Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<35%  
  區間操作(Trading Buy) 5%≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<15%

中立(Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等 
預期近期股價將處於盤整 
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】 
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。  
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。 
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」、「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。 
停止推薦情境： 
1. 達目標價。 
2. 雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。 
3. 推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】 
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line

We introduce 2027 forecast at 90.4K racks (+10% y/y) vs. our 2026 forecast
now at 82K racks: This year is a strong one for downstream rack assemblers, with
rack shipments up ~209% y/y, but we expect rack shipments to grow another ~10%
y/y to reach 90.4K in 2027. For 2027, our forecast implies Hon Hai will be a share
gainer with rack share up to 43% (from 37% in 2026), with Quanta staying
unchanged at 26% and Wistron's share up slightly to 24% (from 23% in 2026).

Quanta (2382.TW, OW): October revenue was NT$353.3bn (+9% m/m, +104% y/y).
We attribute the m/m growth mainly to higher GB200/300 rack shipments at ~1.8K
racks (+24% m/m), despite being offset by lower NB shipments at 2.7M units (-36%
m/m, -23% y/y). For 2026, we forecast Quanta to ship 20.9K racks, and our 2027
rack production forecast for Quanta is at 23.5K (+12% y/y).
Wistron (3231.TW, OW): October revenue was  NT$402.6B (+6% m/m, +108% y/y).
We attribute the m/m growth to: 1) higher GB200/300 compute tray shipments

(1.60K rack equivalents, by our estimates, up ~22% m/m), despite lower Wiwynn
revenue at ~NT$165.1bn (-4% m/m, +81% y/y) and lower NB shipments at 1.2mn units
(-20% m/m, -43% y/y), flat DT shipments at 500K (flat m/m, -44% y/y), and lower
monitor shipments at 850K (-11% m/m, -6% y/y).  For 2026, we forecast Wistron to
ship 18.7K racks, and our 2027 rack production forecast is at 22K (+18% y/y).
Hon Hai (2317.TW, OW): October revenue was NT$1,305.3bn (+13% m/m, +46% y/y),

with consumer segment revenue seeing the strongest m/m growth, followed by
computing products, and lastly, components and other products, while cloud and
networking products were down m/m due to a rise in consignment mix; however,
overall rack production volumes are up m/m. We model HH's October GB200/300
rack production at 2.9K (+7% m/m). For 2026, we forecast Foxconn to ship 30.3K
racks, and our rack production 2027 forecast at 38.8K (+28% y/y).

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
We estimate GB200/300 rack output in October  at ~7.5K (+16% m/m).
Quanta shipped ~1.80K  GB200/GB300 racks.
Wistron shipped ~1.60K GB200/GB300 racks.
Hon Hai shipped ~2.90K GB200/300 racks.
We keep our 2027 rack production forecast unchanged at 90.4K racks (+11% y/y).

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Hon Hai Precision (2317.TW)
Base case scenario value, derived from a residual income valuation model. Our key assump-
tions include a cost of equity of 8.5%, a medium-term growth rate of 13%, and a terminal
growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected iPhone sell-through

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

• Anticipating another strong year into 2027 — MPI has maintained its 
current guidance for capacity plan into next year: year end-2026 capacity 
targets of VPC 2m and MEMS 3.5m, and 2027 MEMS expansion is expected 
to be at least another 2.5m. However, we see potential upside thanks to 
robust customer demand and market share gain. CPO insertion 2 is under 
final evaluation and insertion 3 already starts engineering  shipments  to 
more than 5 international customers across foundry, OSAT and fabless 
segments. The company sees strong growth momentum of probe card into 
next year and CPO equipment contribution at single digit in 2027 with more 
upside into 2028.
• We stay constructive on MPI despite recent market concerns regarding AI 
demand slowing down. The key drivers are continuing to ramp-up at 
existing AI ASIC customers, incremental contributions from new AI/non-AI 
ASIC and CPU customers, further MEMS capacity expansion, and potential 
upside from CPO and microbump probe-card technology.

### Page 2

MPI (6223.TWO)
11 November 2026 Citi Research
2
MPI
Valuation
Our target price for MPI is set at NT$7,200, based on a target PE multiple of 52x applied to our 2027-28E EPS average. We
believe our PE target, at the high end of MPI's 3-year forward PE range, is justified given MPI’s earnings power remains
structurally higher than that in the previous cycles, broadening demand across AI ASIC, additional CPU opportunities and non-
AI ASIC wins. We expect MPI to see accelerating earnings growth ahead supported by sustainable sales growth and an

improving mix. At our target price, the shares would trade at 2027E/28E P/B of 23x/15x.
Risks
Our quant risk rating system rates the MPI stock as High Risk for its share-price volatility. Key downside risks that could
prevent MPI shares from reaching our target price include 1) slowdown in AI ASIC demand; 2) slower-than-expected adoption
of MEMS probe cards; 3) intensifying competition from global and regional peers; 4) slow development of the CPO equipment

business.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do

### Page 1

Micro-Star (2377.TW)
3Q26 Beat on Margins from ASP Pass-Through and Richer Mix
CITI'S TAKE 
MSI reported 3Q26 net profit of NT$4.0bn (+34% QoQ/+138% YoY), 
beating Citi/cons by 27%/45% on stronger-than-expected margins and 
non-op gains. GPM of 17.0% (+1.1ppts QoQ/+5.8 ppts YoY) topped Citi/cons 
at 15.8%/14.5%, as ASP hikes passed through rising component costs while 
moving-avg inventory costs lagged, alongside a richer high-end product 
mix. For 4Q26E, we expect further ASP increases and a rising server 
contribution to largely offset seasonal shipment declines. Into 2027, we 
expect PC demand to stay weak and pass-through room to narrow as 
memory price increases moderately. We raise 2026E-28E earnings and our 
TP to NT$145 (10x 2027E EPS). We stay Neutral as the share price already 
reflects the pass-through upside.
3Q26: Margin-led beat on cost pass-through and better mix — MSI reported 
3Q26 net profit of NT$4.0bn (+34% QoQ/+138% YoY), beating Citi/cons forecasts 
by 27%/45%, mainly on stronger-than-expected margins and non-op gains. GPM 
of 17.0% (+1.1ppts QoQ/+5.8ppts YoY) came in above Citi/cons at 15.8%/14.5%. 
Mgmt. attributed the upside to 1) ASP increase, as MSI raised prices in line with 
suppliers’ cost increases while its moving-average inventory costs rose less; and 2) 
a higher mix of high-end products. Non-op gains, including FX gains of over 
NT$100mn, further boosted the bottom line.
Price hikes to continue; 2027 PC market to keep shrinking — We reiterate our view 
that 4Q26 shipments will decline seasonally from the 3Q26 peak. However, mgmt. 
expects rising raw material costs and continued pass-through to keep lifting ASPs, 
offsetting lower volumes and making the seasonal decline milder than in prior years. 
The server business should also contribute more, potentially reaching a high-single-
digit revenue share. Into 2027, we expect weak PC demand amid elevated costs, 
with MSI’s MB and PC shipments declining by over 10%. Pass-through room should 
narrow as memory price increases moderately, though margins should stay elevated 
for a while, as mgmt. expects memory supply to stay tight in the coming quarters.
Implications — We raise our 2026E-28E earnings forecasts by 6-27%, reflecting 
sustained strong component cost pass-through and higher margin assumptions. 
We adjust TP to NT$145 (from NT$140), implying 10x 2027E EPS (from 12x). We 
believe the share price already reflects MSI’s opportunity to pass component costs 
through to end products, and we expect limited upside going forward, given that the 
PC demand recovery remains highly uncertain. Maintain Neutral.

Micro-Star (2377.TW)

Micro-Star
Company description
Established in 1986 and listed in 1998, Taiwan-based MSI is a leading
OBM/ODM for gaming PCs, motherboards, graphic cards, industrial PCs, and
servers. MSI started business as a motherboard manufacturer but
transformed itself to become a leading gaming PC play in 2012 with a
comprehensive product portfolio across the gaming system and components.
MSI enjoys about 16% global share in the gaming NB industry, and is ranked

No.3 in graphic card shipments and No.3 in motherboard shipments globally.
Investment strategy
We rate MSI shares at Neutral as we anticipate earnings upward pressure
from the delince in PC and MB/VGA shipments in the near-term. While the
soaring component costs could be partially offset by price hike, we expect
muted demand and AI resource crowding to wiegh on margins upside. Further
upside maintains limited, though, until we see a meaningful recovery in core

gaming demand or a more substantial contribution from the server business.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$145 is based on 10x 2027E EPS, below the stock's five-
year average forward 12-month PE. This valuation reflects the earnings
downside risks from muted PC demand and margin pressure from soaring
component costs, while accounting for the time required for the server
business to achieve meaningful scale.
Risks
Key upside risks that could cause MSI's shares to trade above our target price

include: 1) a sharper-than-expected recovery in high-end gaming demand;
and 2) faster-than-anticipated revenue contribution from the AI server
segment. Key downside risks include: 1) a deeper-than-expected contraction
in global PC shipments; 2) prolonged supply shortages for gaming GPUs due
to Ai resource crowding; and 3) inability to pass through surging memory and
CPU costs to price-sensitive consumers.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

We hosted NYPCB’s 3Q26 post result on November 9th. Key 
takeaways include: 
(1) NYPCB mentioned its 4Q26 GM on ABF substrate already 
exceeds 40% (vs. ~15% in 2025 and 25% in 1H26) driven mainly by 
the solid high-end product demand (which contributed 50%+ of the 
ABF revenue in 2026 vs. <20% in 2025), while the company 
mentioned it will gain more high-end market share in 2027 which 
should further drive company’s overall GM level.  
(2) For the new high-end projects in 2027, NYPCB mentioned it will 
gain significant share in the key US CSP next-gen AI project, and 
high-end server CPU project. Also, the company mentioned it 
expects a proactive adoption on 1.6T Ethernet switch demand 
driven by CSPs investment in AI infrastructure, driving its high-end 
switch substrate ASP (the upgrade on substrate, from 800G to 1.6T, 
suggesting a 50%+ higher substrate unit price, per GSe).  
(3) In terms of the ABF substrate pricing, NYPCB believes its ABF 
substrate ASP will further go up by 10-15% QoQ in 4Q26, while it 
also expects the pricing hike ratio to be at least maintained at this 
level from 1Q27-4Q27, without seeing much downside risk. 
Moreover, the company believes the ASP growth could be even 
faster than its expectation if the high-end orders accelerate in 
coming quarters or if they see an even tigher S/D outlook for the 
overall ABF substrate industry.  
(4) In terms of the BT substrate pricing outlook, the company 
mentioned the pricing hike ratio will be low single digits % QoQ in 
4Q26, which will further trend up in 1Q27-4Q27, with a high 
confidence level on the shortage of BT substrate in coming quarters.  
(4) In terms of 4Q26 revenue guidance, the company is expecting 
~20% QoQ revenue growth, driven mainly by the high-end ABF 
projects ramp up + new AI project ramps + BT substrate pricing hike

(low single digits % QoQ); moreover, the company expects its 1Q27 revenue to be flat 
QoQ, despite the ~20% less working days. For GM, the company expects 4Q26 GM to be 
low to mid 30% which should further improve in 1Q27.  
(5) The company mentioned it is working on a new capacity investment plan for 2028, 
but has not yet finalized the plan. In terms of the funding method, NYPCB mentioned it 
will not do any equity based fundraising, but will rely on internal cash or bank loan.  
(6) NYPCB also mentioned it will focus only on CoPoS technology, whose substrate 
pricing is also US$1000+ (vs. US$100-200 for the most high end ABF substrate now), 
while the product demand could start ramping up from 2028 and start contributing 
significant revenue from 2029. For EMIB-T, NYPCB believes the penetration rate could 
only be 10% in the foreseeable future, and it has no plan to invest in this small market.  
We maintain our positive view on the overall ABF substrate industry, with 34%/51% 
shortage ratio in 2027/28 (see here), and believe such high shortage ratio will protect 
companies’ GM/OPM even if we start to see some slowdown on AI CAPEX in coming 
years. We started to see all ABF substrate players’ GM surpass 40% in 4Q26, which is 
much faster than our original expectation (Taiwan ABF substrate suppliers reach 40%+ 
GM at the 6-7th quarters of the shortage in last upcycle, but it only takes 3-4 quarters 
for Taiwan players to hit the 40%+ GM, driven mainly by the solid AI upgrading demand 
this time). We expect NYPCB’s ABF substrate GM could reach historical peak level 
(55-60%) in 2H27, suggesting a fruitful upside for the EPS growth in coming years (we 
expect NYPCB’s EPS to grow by 150%+ 2025-29E CAGR), and believe the ABF substrate 
will continue to be a key bottleneck of the overall semi/tech supply chain in the next 3-5 
years.  
Maintain Buy on NYPCB, as the key spot price ABF substrate supplier in the market 
which should benefit strongly from the S/D shortage and pricing hike in the 
foreseeable future, with an unchanged TP of NT$2,700 (based on 21x 2028E EPS).  
Investment Thesis & PT methodology and risks

Investment Thesis - NYPCB 
NYPCB manufactures and markets printed circuit boards (PCBs) and integrated circuit 
(IC) substrates (including ABF and BT substrates). We are Buy rated on NYPCB 
considering the company’s high exposure to BT substrates and non-LTA ABF substrate 
(70%+ of total revenue), where we expect GM/OPM to increase significantly in the 
coming quarters (from breakeven in OPM in 1H25 to 26/42% in 2026/27E) due to the 
favorable substrate pricing outlook. Despite the company’s low exposure to high-end 
ABF substrate industry (<20% of total revenue in 2025), we believe the expanding 
demand from ASIC AI server and high-end switch IC demand will drive the company’s 
high-end ABF revenue exposure to 40%+ by 2027E, suggesting better GM/OPM outlook 
in the long term. In 2H25-2H26, we believe NYPCB will continue to benefit from stronger 
ABF/BT pricing and should be a key beneficiary of the pricing uptrend considering the 
company’s key focus on BT and non-LTA ABF substrate industries. Moreover, in the long 
term (after 2026), we expect the ABF industry to return to supply tightness, and NYPCB’s 
expanding high-end exposure will lead to a long-term earnings growth outlook (148% 
2026-28E earnings CAGR). As a result, we see solid upside potential for NYPCB’s

Price Target Risks and Methodology - NYPCB

Key downside risks: (1) Slower-than-expected PC demand recovery, (2)
Slower-than-expected ABF substrates pricing upgrading outlook, and (3)
Slower-than-expected NYPCB new high-end capacity qualification progress.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions

on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

研 究 報 告                
穩懋(3105.TT) 
產業：電子–半導體 
2027 光通訊跟衛星本業雙引擎 認列聯茂收益業外動能強勁 
 投資評價與建議 
公司過往Forward PER落在20~30倍，考量光通訊與衛星業務出貨放量，毛利
率迎來結構性跳升，有利估值重估，維持「增加持股」評等。 
 重點評論分析 
3Q26營收符合預期，毛利率因產品組合優化上升至37.4%：3Q26營收58.6億
元，季增11%，營收符合預期主要係因傳統消費性電子旺季，加上航太衛星與
光電產品動能強勁，毛利率37.4%，季增9.2個百分點，主要係因產品組合優化，
航太衛星與光電產品營收占比過半，加上產能利用率上升至75%，業外收入
6.98億元，主因聯茂貢獻業外EPS約1.61元，稅後EPS 4.55元。 
4Q26營運淡季不淡，2027成⻑動能聚焦Optical跟Infrastructure：(1)4Q26
面臨手機傳統淡季與年底例行性歲修，但受惠於PD產能持續開出與其他產品
線營收持平至微幅成⻑，預估4Q26營收將季增1~3%，毛利率37~39%。(2)光
通訊PD於3Q26逐步放量，4Q26將持續成⻑，預估2027年大幅放量，CW Laser
正進行可靠度測試，預估2027年小量貢獻，顯著營收爆發期落在2028年，EML
處於產品開發與測試階段，2028年才有機會產生營收貢獻。(3)航太衛星帶動
Infrastructure成為3Q26季增幅度最高的產品線，衛星佔Infrastructure營收
比重已升至40%，超越傳統基地台。(4)預估聯茂2027年EPS 72.22元，穩懋採
權益法認列持股23%之投資收益約61.7億元，貢獻EPS約14.56元。 
 投資風險 
光通PD跟雷射放量不如預期、衛星發射遞延。

### Page 2

2 
| 營運概況 
3Q26 產品組合優化帶動毛利率與 EPS 創高：3Q26 營收 58.6 億元，季增 11%，營收符合預期主要係因傳統消費
性電子旺季，加上航太衛星與光電產品動能強勁，毛利率 37.4%，季增 9.2 個百分點，優於財測，主要係因產品
組合優化，航太衛星與光電產品營收占比過半，加上產能利用率上升至 75%，營業利益率 24.2%，業外收入 6.98
億元，因持有聯茂 23%股權並採權益法認列投資收益，聯茂貢獻業外 EPS 約 1.61 元，稅後 EPS 4.55 元。 
4Q26 營運淡季不淡，2027 成⻑動能聚焦 Optical 跟 Infrastructure：(1)儘管 4Q26 面臨手機傳統淡季與年底例
行性歲修，但受惠於 PD 產能持續開出與其他產品線營收持平至微幅成⻑，預估 4Q26 營收將季增 1~3%，毛利
率 37~39%，主要受惠產品組合優化跟產能利用率維持 70%以上。(2)中階手機受臨中國 PA 供應鏈本土化及記憶
體缺貨的逆風，但穩懋聚焦中高階手機市場，動能維持穩健，4Q26 Cellular 營收僅呈現小幅下滑，WiFi 產品在
Router 需求回流下，3Q26 雙位數成⻑，4Q26 呈現持平至微幅成⻑。(3)光通訊 PD 於 3Q26 逐步放量，4Q26
將持續成⻑，預估 2027 年產能完全開出並大幅放量，CW Laser 正進行可靠度測試，預估 2027 年小量貢獻，顯
著營收爆發期落在 2028，EML 處於產品開發與測試階段，2028 年才有機會產生營收貢獻。(4)航太衛星帶動
Infrastructure 成為 3Q26 季增幅度最高的產品線，衛星佔 Infrastructure 營收比重已升至 40%，超越傳統基地
台，目前 GaN 月產能是 300 片左右，公司積極擴充 GaN 產能，預計 1Q27 導入 GaN 6 吋製程生產。(5)聯茂
2027 年爆發性成⻑，預估聯茂 2027 年 EPS 72.22 元，穩懋採權益法認列持股 23%之投資收益約 61.7 億元，貢
獻EPS約14.56元。

| 公 司 簡 介 
穩懋(3105)為全球最大的化合物半導體晶圓代工廠，核心業務聚焦於微波通訊與光電元件的砷化鎵(GaAs)及氮化
鎵(GaN)晶圓代工服務，公司總部及研發中心設於台灣桃園龜山(華亞科技園區)，主要六吋晶圓生產基地位於桃
園華亞廠及龜山廠，總產能約 4.3 萬片，3Q26 產能利用率為 75%。3Q26 年營收比重為 Cellular(手機
PA)25~30%、Infrastructure(通訊基礎建設)35~40%、Wi-Fi 15~20%、Optical 10~15%、其他 3%。主要客
戶包含 Broadcom、Lumentum、Murata 等國際大廠，銷售地區為台灣 8%、亞洲 70%、美洲 15%、歐洲
7%。 
穩懋的微波通訊產品處理高頻訊號的放大與切換，主要元件為異質接面雙載子電晶體(HBT)與假晶高電子遷移率
電晶體(pHEMT) ，用於智慧型手機功率放大器(PA)、Wi-Fi 路由器及低軌衛星設備的射頻(RF)訊號，提供低雜

3Q26 Review: EV 판매 증가의 역풍 
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 160,000 원(유지) 
 현재주가 95,600 원(10/30)

 동사의 3분기 실적은 매출액 31.6조원(+10.1%yoy), 영업이익 1.8조원
(+24.3%yoy, OPM 5.8%), 지배이익 1.29조원(-9.8%yoy)을 기록, 도매 판
매 증가로 인한 매출 증가에도 불구하고 인센티브/원자재 비용 증가로 인해 
컨센서스를 하회하는 실적 쇼크를 기록 
 영업이익 증감 요인: 인센티브&가격 -7,270억원, 믹스 -4,650억원, 재료비 
-3,680억원으로 총 1.56조원 비용 발생. 증가 요인은 판매량 +4,420억원, 
환율 +5,290억원, 기타비용 절감 +4,130억원으로 1.38조원 상쇄. 인센티
브로 인한 비용 증가 예상외로 컸는데 EV 판매량 증가 영향. 기말환율 하락
으로 판매보증충당금은 전년비 9,254억원 감소 
 전동화 믹스 증가: 글로벌 xEV 소매 판매 30만대(+47%yoy), 판매비중 
36.2%(+9.8%p yoy). HEV 17.2만대(+46%yoy), 텔루라이드, 스포티지 
HEV 공급 확대로 3분기 미국 HEV 판매 +118%yoy 기록. EV 12.3만대
(+75%yoy), 서유럽 EV 판매는 +163%yoy 기록 
 4분기 전망: 중동 분쟁 지속으로 원가 부담의 급격한 개선은 어려울 전망. 
연초 CID에서 제시한 연간 판매 목표는 달성 가능할 것으로 보이지만 기존 
수익성 목표를 하회할 전망. 중장기적으로 상품 경쟁력 강화, 지역별 파워트
레인 대응 전략 유지, 내부 원가 및 체질 개선으로 외부 비용 부담 상쇄를 
추진할 계획. 중동 분쟁 완화에 따른 비용 부담 해소와 HEV 비중 확대를 통
한 수익성 개선은 2027년 시점이 될 것으로 예상 
 단기 방향 전환 어려울 듯: 판매 호조에도 불구하고 저가형 EV 판매 비중 
증가함에 따라 수익성이 악화되는 모습이 3분기에도 재확인. 글로벌 수요 
변화를 감안했을 때, EV 시장 선점이 매우 중요한 것은 사실이지만 단기 수
익성 부담 커지고 있는 점 우려됨. 중국산 EV의 글로벌 확산, 중동 전쟁 장
기화에 따른 원가 부담 증대가 연말까지는 영향을 미칠 것. 동사에 대한 투
자의견 매수와 목표주가 16만원 유지함

시가총액(십억원) 37,323

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에도 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

투자기간 및 투자등급/투자의견 비율

### Page 1

기아 (000270)

EARNINGS REVIEW Company Analysis | 자동차/로봇 | 2026.11.02  
잠시 쉬어갑니다

3Q26 Review 
기아는 3Q26P 매출액 31.6조원(+10.1% YoY, -4.4% QoQ), 영업이익 1.8조원
(+24.4% YoY, -30.8% QoQ, OPM 5.8%)을 기록, 컨센서스를 하회했다. 영업이익 증
감 요인은 환율 효과(+5,290억원), 물량 효과(+4,420억원), 믹스 효과(-4,650억원), 
인센티브/가격 효과(-7,270억원), 재료비 영향(-3,680억원), 기타(+4,130억원)이다. 
판매 성장에도 높아진 수익성 부담 
글로벌 도매 판매 84.7만대(+7.9% 이하 YoY), 중국 제외 판매량은 83.1만대
(+8.8%)를 기록했다. 미국 HEV와 서유럽 EV 판매가 각각 118%, 163% 증가하며 
글로벌 점유율은 4.0%까지 확대됐다. 다만, 판매 성장에도 원화 강세와 원자재 가격 
상승, EV 비중 확대에 따른 믹스 부담으로 수익성은 전분기 대비 하락했다. 유럽 내 
EV 판매가 늘어나면서 믹스가 악화되었으며, 전쟁으로 인한 원자재 상승으로 3분기 
재료비 부담은 전년 대비 약 3,700억 증가했다.  
연간 판매목표 335만대는 유지했으나, 4분기 수익성의 급격한 반등은 어려울 전망이
다. 향후 미국 텔루라이드·스포티지 HEV 공급 확대와 유럽 EV 판매 증가에 따른 고정
비 흡수가 수익성 회복의 핵심이다. 
투자의견은 Buy를 유지하고 목표주가는 155,000원으로 하향 
기아에 대한 투자의견 Buy를 유지하고, 실적 추정치 하향을 반영해 목표주가를 
155,000원으로 하향한다. 재료비 부담, 환율 효과 축소, 예상보다 큰 믹스 악화로 단
기 이익 눈높이 조정은 불가피하다. 다만 산업수요 둔화에도 주요 시장에서 점유율을 
확대되고 있다는 점은 긍정적이다. 현재 배당 수익률은 7% 수준으로, 상대적으로 높
은 배당 매력은 주가 하방을 지지할 전망이다. 
 Analyst 이병근 
bglee@ls-sec.co.kr 
Buy (유지)

Document 252: [005930] 삼성전자(005930) STRESS TEST삼성전자(005930) STRESS TESTSTRESS TEST - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005930] 삼성전자(005930) STRESS TEST삼성전자(005930) STRESS TESTSTRESS TEST - 기업.pdf

### Page 1

반도체(Overweight): Stress Test  
78 
삼성전자  (005930) 
2026.11.04

STRESS TEST 
[반도체/전기전자] 고의영 
2122-9179 ey.ko@imfnsec.com

목표주가 37만원, ‘28년 기준으로는 56만원까지 열림 
투자의견 Buy, 목표주가 37만원으로 커버리지를 개시한다. ‘27년 BPS에 목표 
P/B 2.3배를 적용했다. 이는 ‘00년 이후 25년간 평균 P/B의 +2표준편차에 
해당한다[표1]. ‘26~’28년 ROE는 각각 56%, 59%, 48%로 지난 25년 평균인 
22%를 크게 상회할 전망이며, 고원형 ROE가 장기간 유지될 것으로 판단한다.  
이익 지속성에 대한 믿음이 공고해질 경우 시장의 시야는 ‘28년으로 향할 것이다. 
이 경우 ‘28년 BPS 기준 목표주가는 56만원까지 열릴 수 있다[표2]. 
동사 주가는 ‘26년 6월 고점 대비 10월 저점 기준 43% 하락해 과거 다운사이클 
평균 하락폭인 -41%를 넘어섰다. 특히 과거 8~13개월에 걸쳐 진행됐던 조정이 
이번에는 4개월 만에 나타났다. 시장이 ‘28년 이익 급감을 상정하고 있는 것이다. 
이러한 우려가 지속되는 한, 이익의 눈높이가 높아질수록 멀티플은 저렴해질 
뿐이며, DRAM 가격이 예상보다 더 올라갈 것이라는 소식이 주가의 촉매로 
작용하지 못한다. 결국 주가 반등을 위해서는 시장이 상정하고 있는 다운사이클이 
우려만큼 깊지 않을 것이라는 믿음이 필요한 것으로 본다.

STRESS TEST와 HBM 재평가 
① 당사는 고객들이 어느 정도 가격대에서 LTA를 해지하려는 유인이 생길지 
추정해봤다. Micron의 코멘트를 차용하여 ‘22~’23년 수준의 스트레스 테스트를 
시나리오 분석한 결과, 4Q27 고점 대비 DRAM 가격이 60% 수준 빠져야 해지가 
검토될 수 있다[그림2]. 그마저도 다음 공급 부족기의 물량 배정 우선순위 상실 
등 정성적 요인까지 감안하면 실제 해지 가능성은 더욱 낮은 것으로 판단한다. 
② HBM 비중이 높아질수록 동사의 이익 변동성은 낮아질 것이다. 특히 
중장기적으로 확대될 cHBM은 고객별 수주생산 제품으로 현물시장과 유통 
재고가 없고, 생산에 앞서 물량과 가격이 결정된다. 수요가 둔화되더라도 범용 
메모리처럼 재고 누적과 가격 급락으로 이어지기보다는 발주 조정이나 계약 
재협상을 통해 영향을 받는 구조다.  
동사 HBM 매출은 ‘26년 28조원에서 ‘28년 215조원으로 증가할 전망이다. 
‘27년 HBM 가격 방향성의 윤곽이 잡힌 현 시점에서, 동사 HBM 매출의 변수는 
가격이 아니라 물량이며, 물량은 고객과 사전에 협의되는 구조다. 과거 메모리 
업황에서는 2년 뒤의 이익을 가늠하기 어려웠으나, 이제는 HBM을 통해 중장기 
실적을 논의할 수 있게 됐다는 점, 그 자체에 주목한다. 
만약 동사의 ‘26~’28년 HBM 영업이익에 시기별 TSMC P/E (21배, 15배, 12배)를 
적용하면 HBM 가치는 동사 시가총액의 19%, 69%, 108%를 설명한다[표3]. 
특히 cHBM이 본격화할 ‘28년에는 HBM만으로 현재 시가총액이 설명될 수 
있다는 의미다. 시장이 통상 받아들이는 10배를 타겟팅해도 결과는 크게 다르지 
않다. 
(계속) 
Buy (Initiation)

반도체(Overweight): Stress Test  
79

‘27년 주가 경로: 회복, 박스권, 재평가 
연말까지는 급락 이후 저점을 높여가는 회복 구간일 전망이다.  
‘27년 상반기까지는 ‘28년 업황 지속성에 대한 우려가 상존할 전망이다. 이 불확실성이 
멀티플을 누르는 동안 ‘27년 이익은 계속 상향되므로 EPS 상승과 멀티플 하락이 상쇄되는 
구간이 이어질 전망이다. 기회비용은 결국 환원수익률로 보상받아야 하므로, ‘27년 1월 신규 
주주환원 정책이 중요한 변곡점이 될 것으로 판단한다.  
당사는 동사가 ‘27~’29년 FCF의 50%를 주주에게 환원하고, 이 중 70%를 배당, 30%를 
자사주 매입/소각으로 쓰는 것으로 가정하여, 현재 주가 기준 ‘27년 15% 수준의 
주주환원수익률을 기대한다. 미국 10년물 국채 금리가 5% 안팎인 환경에서 무위험 금리의 
3배에 달하는 환원수익률, 그중 배당만으로 10% 수준의 수익률은 그 자체로 희소한 투자 
포인트로 볼 수 있다. 
‘27년 하반기 이후에는, 실적 추정의 기준 연도가 ‘28년으로 넘어가고, 이후 신규 팹 물량이 
소량 출하되면서 센티먼트상 우려는 커질 것이다. 그러나 LTA가 이익을 지킨다는 사실이 
숫자로 확인되고 cHBM의 가치가 재평가된다면 순환 할인이 축소될 것으로 판단한다.

업사이드 요인 
① AI 투자 지속성 우려로 최근 주가 하락이 가팔랐던 만큼, 이 부분에 대한 우려가 
완화되면 반등의 속도와 폭이 예상보다 빠를 수 있다 
② 향후 주주환원 규모가 예상보다 더 적극적일 경우 추가적인 업사이드가 될 수 있다. 
혹은 주주환원 이후에도 남는 대규모 FCF의 활용 방안도 촉매가 될 수 있다.

 회사는 해당 종목을 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 금융투자분석사와 그 배우자는 해당 기업의 주식을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

[투자의견] 
종목추천 투자등급  
종목투자의견은 향후 12개월간 추천일 종가대비 해당종목의 예상 목표수익률을 의미함.  
 Buy(매수): 추천일 종가대비 +15% 이상  
 Hold(보유): 추천일 종가대비 -15% ~ 15% 내외 등락 
 Sell(매도): 추천일 종가대비 -15% 이상

Document 294: AI基建股蒸发4.2万亿美元，“卖铲盛宴”终结？

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI基建股蒸发4.2万亿美元，“卖铲盛宴”终结？.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-16
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

6 ⽉以来，卡特彼勒跌 44% 、 Oklo 暴跌 49% 。分析师直指根源：投资者将传统⼯业股当成
⻓股炒作，估值严重虚⾼。此次抛售的另⼀重要背景，是美国中期选举后数据中⼼监管政
策⾛向的不明朗。基本⾯仍具韧性 —— 卡特彼勒积压订单达 780 亿美元，多家企业营收增⻓
已锁定⾄ 2027 年。
摘要：
全球制造、公⽤事业与矿业企业⾃今年 6 ⽉以来市值合计蒸发 4.2 万亿美元，这场抛售⼏乎抹去了
此前 12 个⽉内同类企业所积累的 9.4 万亿美元涨幅的近半数，曾⻛靡⼀时的 AI“ 卖铲 ” 交易正在急
速逆转。
据英国《⾦融时报》分析，在媒体报道中与数据中⼼建设关联度越⾼的企业，遭受的冲击越为
惨烈。投资者对 AI 热潮的信⼼正在动摇，从适配超⼤规模数据中⼼的燃⽓发电机组制造商，到连
接 AI 服务器机架所需的⾼端光纤企业，此前被争相追捧的标的如今纷纷遭到抛售。⼯业板块已成
为今夏以来标普 500 指数中表现第⼆差的板块，仅次于科技板块。
市场的转向折射出更⼴泛的担忧：超⼤规模云计算企业持续以债务融资推⾼资本开⽀，叠加美

国国内对数据中⼼建设⽇益⾼涨的政治阻⼒，令投资者开始质疑这场建设热潮能否持续。
⻰头企业市值腰斩
数据中⼼燃⽓发电机组的主要供应商卡特彼勒（ Caterpillar ）过去六个⽉跌幅超过 30% ，⽽⾃ 6
⽉ 30 ⽇股价触及历史⾼点以来，市值已缩⽔ 2140 亿美元，跌幅达 44% 。
此外，核电企业 Oklo 尽管在今年 1 ⽉与 Meta 签署了供电合同，股价仍重挫 49% 。暖通空调系统制
造商 Comfort Systems 与光纤制造商康宁（ Corning ）在同⼀时期均损失了约⼀半市值。
据英国⾦融时报称，德意志银⾏⾼级美国经济学家 Brett Ryan 表⽰，此前有⼤量机构资⾦ “ 盲⽬
涌⼊ ” 这些交易， “ 可能助推了部分企业过于乐观的估值 ”。他还指出，股票市场正在 “ 向信贷市
场早已发出的信号靠拢 ”—— 后者对数据中⼼建设中⻛险融资的增⻓早有警⽰。
⻛险投资⼈、⿇省理⼯学院研究员 Paul Kedrosky 则直⾔，投资者将传统⼯业企业当作半导体股
票来对待，本⾝就是⼀个错误。

⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

不过，尽管股价承压，多家企业的经营基本⾯依然稳健。美国政府最新数据显⽰，截⾄今年 9 ⽉
的 12 个⽉内，美国数据中⼼建设⽀出（不含 IT 设备与芯⽚）达到 960 亿美元。
第三季度业绩同样印证了需求的韧性。卡特彼勒报告积压订单达创纪录的 780 亿美元，其发电业
务销售额同⽐增⻓ 75% ，主要由数据中⼼需求驱动。西⻔⼦来⾃数据中⼼的电⽓设备订单积压
在 2026 年增⻓ 60% ，达到 70 亿欧元；伊顿（ Eaton ）同类订单则实现同⽐翻倍。多家燃⽓轮机
制造商表⽰， 2026 年前九个⽉已斩获数⼗亿美元订单。
Dell'Oro Group 分析师 Alex Cordovil 表⽰， “ 就这些企业的营收基本⾯和潜在盈利前景⽽⾔，我
们仍然看到⾮常强劲的预期。 ” 他补充道，电⽓化和供应链重构等更宏观的趋势持续⽀撑着众多⼯
业企业的核⼼业务， “ 建设并未减速 …… 积压订单处于历史⾼位，这意味着⾄少到 2027 年，营收
增⻓已基本锁定。 ”
⼯业控制设备制造商 Allient ⾸席执⾏官 Richard S Warzala 在 11 ⽉ 5 ⽇的业绩电话会上表⽰，对 AI

的需求不会下滑。
此外， Kedrosky 对订单积压数据持保留态度，认为这是滞后指标，反映的是此前的预期，⽽⾮
当下的真实需求。他指出，企业存在 " 双重乃⾄三重下单 " 的现象 —— 为了在感知到的供应紧缺中
抢占排队位置⽽超额备货。
Brett Ryan 的判断则更为直接：股票市场正在完成⼀次迟来的估值校正，向信贷市场早已传递的
⻛险信号对⻬。这场历时数⽉、市值蒸发逾 4 万亿美元的调整，本质上是资本市场对 " ⼀切皆成⻓
股 " 叙事的系统性重新定价。
中期选举后的监管不确定性
此次抛售的另⼀重要背景，是美国中期选举后数据中⼼监管政策⾛向的不明朗。在本⽉的中期
选举中，对数据中⼼建设实施限制的提案获得了美国选⺠的⼴泛⽀持，但其实际影响⽬前仍不
明朗。
花旗研究在选举后发布的报告中指出，尽管预计更严格的监管将给数据中⼼⾏业带来更多波
动，但⼤范围限制建设的可能性仍然较低。
星座能源（ Constellation Energy ）⾸席执⾏官 Joseph Dominguez 表⽰，选举后的市场反应是

“ 过度反应 ” ，并强调⺠主党⼈ “ ⾮常在意 ” 保护数据中⼼的蓝领就业岗位。
德意志银⾏的 Ryan 则认为，即便部分地区出台禁令，也只会将建设需求转移⾄监管更为宽松的
地区。
“ 在与对冲基⾦和家族办公室的讨论中， ‘ ⼀切都是成⻓股 ’ 这种论调⽆处不在 —— 这当然是荒谬
的。 ”
“ 即便对新数据中⼼的建设实施暂停令，那些已经开⼯的项⽬也必须完成。 ”
“ 归根结底，如果市场对这些设施存在需求，它们终将在某个地⽅建成。 ”

### Page 3

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

Document 339: CNB Minutes_ Neither hikes nor cuts in the pipeline

Source PDF: all report pdf/CNB Minutes_ Neither hikes nor cuts in the pipeline.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-13
Pages: 8
Status: ok

### Page 1

The Bank Board increased interest rates to 4% in a close call,
and we see valid arguments for both positions. Second-round
eﬀects will push inﬂation above the upper bound of the target
corridor for some time, while dwindling economic activity
warrants lots of caution. We see neither hikes nor cuts as the
most likely outcome for quite some time
David Havrlant
The Czech National Bank's decision to raise rates on 5 November was a close
call
Assessing the impact of Hormuz

The Czech National Bank increased the policy rate by 25bp to 4% in
a split vote on 5 November, with Governor Ales Michl and board
members Jan Kubicek, Jan Prochazka and Jakub Seidler voting for
a hike. Meanwhile, Deputy Governors Eva Zamrazilova and Jan Frait,
along with board member Karina Kubelkova, favoured interest rate
stability. We assess the current situation and the risks to the
outlook as complex and see relevant justiﬁcation for both options,

depending on whether your concerns are more on the side of

### Page 2

second-round inﬂationary eﬀects or on the side of disappointing
economic activity and the consequences for consumption.
The Bank Board assessed overall risks as pro-inﬂationary. There
was an agreement that the secondary impacts of the energy shock
have not been signiﬁcantly reﬂected in domestic inﬂation so far.
However, as the Hormuz conﬂict becomes more protracted, the
likelihood of tangible second-round eﬀects increases. Jakub Seidler
noted that global energy prices have been elevated for too long, so

it would be diﬃcult to avoid a pass-through to other segments of
consumer prices. Deputy Governor Zamrazilova emphasised that
much depends on further developments in the Middle Eastern
conﬂict. Should tensions ease in the near future, a rate hike at the
November meeting would not be necessary. The key question is
whether the conﬂict will eventually pass or become the new
normal, with the latter implying persistently high energy costs.
Deputy Governor Frait stated that energy prices remaining at these

levels for such an extended period could have a negative impact on
demand and economic activity, mainly by reducing the real
incomes of households and companies. The current negative
supply shock has hit the Czech economy from a favourable starting
position, but the factors that helped keep inﬂation close to the
target this year are about to fade. The increase in longer-term rates
contributes to the restrictive stance of monetary policy, and implies

some softening in domestic lending activity. The board members
agreed that this is a key factor reducing the need for further
tightening for the time being.
To rise or not to rise?
However, as Jan Kubicek noted, core inﬂation is not slowing down
as previously assumed and is even slightly increasing. This
represents a fundamental concern, especially in combination with
the disappointing real growth performance. That said, producer
prices have embarked on an upward trend and may put further

pressure on the core rate. Jakub Seidler and Karina Kubelkova
emphasised that the renewed price increases in the energy and
food segment are usually the key drivers when it comes to inﬂation

### Page 3

expectations, and the ﬁrst signs of an increase are already visible.
And we all know that unanchored inﬂation expectations are one of
the things that keep central bankers awake at night.
Producer prices will drive consumer prices
Source: CZSO, ING, Macrobond
En gros, among the main reasons for a rate hike in November were:
i) persistent core inﬂation, ii) still robust wage dynamics and iii)
second-round eﬀects expected to bite soon. Among the main

reasons for staying on hold and evaluating were: i) secondary
inﬂationary impacts are so far limited, ii) long-term rates deliver
restriction, iii) disappointing economic activity.
We see the arguments in both directions as valid, which made the
November decision relatively complex and was reﬂected in the
close vote. Time will tell whether the second-round eﬀects that we
believe are just kicking in will prove signiﬁcant without weighing too
heavily on economic activity. Or whether the deteriorating real

purchasing power and dwindling real wage growth could lead to
weaker household consumption, which would in turn put
downward pressure on inﬂation. Both eﬀects are certainly
interconnected, which only underscores the beauty of
macroeconomics, and the formidable power of prices to clear the
market.

### Page 4

Punchy inﬂation across price segments
When it comes to our inﬂation forecast, we deﬁnitely see pro-
inﬂationary pressures when looking ahead. We expect annual
producer price growth to peak at around 7% in industry early next
year and at around 12% in agriculture mid-next year. At the same
time, the annual pace of import prices already quickened to above
8% in September. Those are not low growth rates, and all are going
to feed into consumer prices sooner or later. At the same time, the

koruna continues to weaken against the dollar, making imports
pricier. With all that on board, we see the yearly average of inﬂation
picking up from 2.1% this year to 3.2% next, reaching a peak of just
below 4% in February.
Inﬂation to hover above the target for
longer
Source: CNB, ING, Macrobond
The most pronounced uncertainty lies with January’s gain in
regulated prices. We have already collected information from
distributors and factor in a 3.5% increase in electricity prices, a

6.5% increase in natural gas prices, an 8% increase in heating
prices and a 6% rise in water charges. Nonetheless, not all
distributors will apply the same increase and not all households will
be aﬀected equally. Based on our best estimate, we expect a 3%
monthly increase in regulated prices in January, resulting in a 3.8%

### Page 5

year-on-year gain. However, should global oil and European natural
gas prices remain elevated, we expect further increases throughout
the year, lifting average regulated price growth to 4.5% next year.
This ﬁgure is still somewhat below the CNB's 5.6% forecast, which
seems to be at the upper bound of what we currently deem
plausible.
Regulated and food prices turn pro-
inﬂationary
Source: CNB, ING, Macrobond
Food prices are set to shift from an anti-inﬂationary position this

year to an inﬂationary one, averaging just below 3% next year, but
peaking at around 4%. That said, we sense quite some upward risks
here, especially should energy prices stay elevated for longer, which
would propel fertiliser prices and transport costs.
Neither hikes nor cuts in the pipeline
When it comes to core inﬂation, two opposing forces are set for a
duel: second-round eﬀects, coupled with rising import prices,
against a softening housing market and crumbling real purchasing

power. Timing is of the essence. We expect the former to strike ﬁrst,
but the latter to respond without much hesitation. For now, we see
the forces as roughly balanced, keeping average core inﬂation just
below 3% next year.

### Page 6

Consumption could slow tangibly along with
real wages
Source: CZSO, ING, Macrobond
So yes, upbeat inﬂation across price segments warrants restrictive
monetary policy. Meanwhile, weakening economic activity
warrants a lot of caution when it comes to any future rate hikes.
The thing is, we see real GDP growth averaging just 1.7% this year
and lifting oﬀ only gradually to 2.1% next year. Meanwhile, we see
household consumption slowing tangibly to 1.8% next year from

2.5% this year. Indeed, we are likely positioned at the lower end of
the forecast range, yet we take the view that a slowdown in real
wage growth will eventually take its toll. Therefore, we see neither
further hikes nor cuts when looking way ahead, with the 4% policy
rate settling in as the new normal.
The 4% rate makes itself at home

It would be a diﬀerent story, however, should we see another round
of escalation in the current conﬂicts, pushing Brent crude back

towards US$120/bbl. In that case, the path to a 4.5% CNB base rate
is pretty straightforward, if it pleases. If this were to happen, the
EUR/CZK would likely lend a helping hand to mitigate the imported
inﬂationary pressures from the eurozone, as the European Central
Bank seems constrained in this respect, not least by elevated French
bond yields. In contrast, should we slip into an episode of stagnant
growth in Europe for whatever reason, our hypothesis of a 4%

nominal interest rate becoming the new normal would start to look
a little shakier, at least temporarily.

Document 355: ECONOMIC WEEK AHEAD_ November 16-20

Source PDF: all report pdf/ECONOMIC WEEK AHEAD_ November 16-20.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-11-15
Pages: 8
Status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: ECONOMIC WEEK AHEAD: November 16-20
Date:    7 hours ago
ECONOMIC WEEK AHEAD: November 16-20

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
ECONOMIC WEEK AHEAD:
November 16-20
By Ed Yardeni & Toby Hearst • 14 Nov 2026
View in browser

### Page 2

Photo by Monica Sauro / Unsplash

### Page 3

Last week’s inflation data ran hot. October’s headline CPI rose 0.4% m/m,
while the core CPI rose 0.3% for a third straight month. The PPI for final
demand rose 0.3% m/m in October after a revised 1.4% in September,
lifting the y/y rate to 6.5%, the highest since November 2022.
This week’s calendar is busy, with the FOMC minutes, Nvidia’s earnings,
Anthropic’s IPO, retail sales, and industrial production. Here’s more:
(1) FOMC minutes & FedSpeak. Minutes of the October 27-28 FOMC

meeting (Wed) should show how many of the 19 participants (12 voters
and 7 nonvoters) leaned toward a rate hike. The Committee held the
federal funds rate (FFR) target range at 3.75%-4.00% in a 10-2 vote, with
Fed Governor Lisa Cook and Cleveland Fed President Beth Hammack
dissenting in favor of a 25bps increase.
Six Fed officials speak this week (Warsh, Williams, Musalem, Hammack,
Goolsbee, and Paulson). Fed Chair Kevin Warsh appears Monday at the

Fed’s AI and productivity task force hearing in the San Francisco Bay
Area. New York Fed President John Williams, who speaks Monday and
Wednesday, said on Thursday that “inflation is far too high” and that
further tightening will be needed. We think the Fed should and will hike by
50bps in December.
FFR futures imply 3.6 rate hikes of 25 bps over the next 12 months
including 2.6 over the next six months (chart). The odds of a 50bps hike at
the December meeting are just 11.5% currently.

### Page 4

The 2-year Treasury bond yield is at 4.86%, well above the 4.00% top of
the FFR target range, with 12-month futures at 4.78% (chart).
(2) Nvidia earnings & Anthropic IPO. Nvidia reports Q3 earnings on
Tuesday, its first report since Jensen Huang’s resignation as CEO.
Revenue is expected to be $109.1 billion, up from $57.0 billion a year
earlier and slightly above the company’s $108 billion guidance. The
stock’s forward P/E is down to 11.5 (chart). The conference call with

interim CEO Colette Kress, who remains CFO, is likely to be more
revealing than the numbers.

### Page 5

Anthropic shares are expected to begin trading Thursday, with a price
range of $70-$78. That implies a fully diluted valuation of $1.26 trillion to
$1.40 trillion. We feel confident about the company’s prospects based on
its S-1 filing.
(3) Retail sales. October retail sales (Tue) follow September’s 0.8% m/m
gain, with sales ex autos up 0.9%. Retail sales ex food services rose 6.2%
y/y in September, and Redbook same-store sales continue to run well

ahead of the official measure (chart).
(4) Manufacturing. November’s NY and Philly Fed surveys (Mon, Thu)
follow solid October readings. Their average edged up to 23.2 in October
from 22.7 in September. The average has run well ahead of
manufacturing production growth this year, which was just 1.4% y/y in

### Page 6

September (chart). Historically, the two have tracked more closely,
suggesting that factory output could accelerate in the months ahead.
October’s industrial production (Tue) follows September’s 0.7% m/m gain,
which lifted the index to 104.0. Capacity utilization was 76.9%.
Manufacturing aggregate weekly hours slipped to 84.0 in October from
84.2 in September, suggesting that factory output weakened last month
(chart).
(5) ADP & unemployment claims. ADP’s weekly employment reading

(Tue) will update a four-week average that has been rising for the past two
weeks (chart).

### Page 7

Initial jobless claims (Thu) follow last week’s 200,000, up from 189,000,
with the four-week average at 195,250 (chart). Continuing claims fell to
1,697,000 for the week ended October 30, with the four-week average
steady at 1,709,000.

Document 369: FX Daily_ Time for a break below 1.10 in EUR_USD_

Source PDF: all report pdf/FX Daily_ Time for a break below 1.10 in EUR_USD_.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-16
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

EUR/USD is ready to test the key 1.100 support. We think the
pair can hold the line in a light week for US data, but risks of a
break lower are meaningful given the macro nature of this USD
rally. Any AI-related equity market instability probably has a
greater chance of denting this USD momentum than Fedspeak
this week

Francesco Pesole, Frantisek Taborsky and Chris Turner
EUR/USD could test 1.10 if upcoming data provides further support for the
dollar
Please see ING's FX Outlook 2027: Fat tails, slim ranges for
insights into key themes next year.
USD: Strong momentum looks harder to
unwind
The macro backdrop makes it risky to call for a dollar correction just
yet. Two-year USD swap rates have risen 35bp since the Federal

### Page 2

Reserve's Christopher Waller mentioned a 50bp hike and core CPI
overshot on 10 November, while other Fed speakers have done little
to quell the hawkish repricing. We’d be more bearish on the dollar if
markets had jumped to pricing a 50bp move in December, which
seems unlikely. Instead, the Fed funds futures curve favours back-
to-back 25bp increases in December and January. That looks not
only more realistic but, crucially, considerably harder for moderate

FOMC members to push back against than an imminent 50bp
move.
We therefore do not see this week’s long list of Fed speakers as a
signiﬁcant downside risk for the dollar. Chair Kevin Warsh speaks
today, but he’s been wary of oﬀering guidance, especially outside
of meetings, so we wouldn't be surprised to get very little from his
remarks. The minutes from the October FOMC meeting are due on
Wednesday. The recent hawkish turn in Fedspeak and hotter core

CPI have probably raised the bar for a market-impactful surprise.
The larger risk of a USD correction comes from US equities, which
have shown great potential to drive dovish repricing. The AI trade
faces two major tests this week: Nvidia reports quarterly earnings
tomorrow, while Anthropic is expected to make its stock market
debut on Thursday. So far, tech stocks have held up remarkably
well despite Nvidia’s idiosyncratic selloﬀ, and futures currently point

to a modestly positive start to the week.
Today’s Empire Manufacturing index is one of the few notable data
inputs this week. Given the size of the front-end rates move and the
arguably high bar for further hawkish surprises, we tend to favour
dollar stabilisation rather than another leg higher. But for now, we
think markets will tend to fade dollar corrections.
Francesco Pesole
EUR: 1.100 test coming
With the exception of tomorrow’s ZEW release, the eurozone macro

calendar is mostly centred on central bank speakers this week,
including outgoing ECB President Christine Lagarde on Thursday
and Friday. While the European Central Bank's rhetoric has

### Page 3

remained broadly hawkish-leaning, the contrast with Fed members’
comments is stark.
That divergence is mirrored in the EUR/USD 2-year swap rate
diﬀerential, which has widened by nearly 35bp to its widest since
September 2025. EUR/USD was some 5-6% higher back then, but
the backdrop couldn’t be more diﬀerent. The dollar was still
suﬀering from a material post-Liberation Day risk premium. Now, it
is the euro facing some premium due to heightened eurozone ﬁscal

risk. More importantly, the real front-end rate diﬀerential is
signiﬁcantly lower. Adjusted for 2yr inﬂation swaps, it is at its
widest since December 2022.
Our macro team still thinks markets are overestimating US inﬂation
risks and will face large dovish repricing, but that doesn’t seem a
very near-term story. Given the lighter US calendar, we feel EUR/USD
can hold the line at 1.100 this week, but risks of a break lower are
signiﬁcant, especially as that would be only a small stretch relative

to short-term fundamentals. For now, EUR/USD optimists like us
should be content with some stabilisation in the 1.10-1.11 range.
Francesco Pesole
CAD: Inﬂation shouldn't endorse a
December hike
Canadian inﬂation data for October is released today. Headline CPI
should accelerate by 0.3% month-on-month, as in the US, taking
the yearly rate slightly higher from 3.1% to 3.2%. That won’t
matter too much unless the so far extra-stable core measures start

to climb. Expectations are for only a small 0.1ppt tick higher in
median and trim CPI, with both staying anchored around 2.0%.
Don’t expect the Bank of Canada to turn particularly more hawkish
on this inﬂation backdrop. December remains a risk for a surprise
hike (15bp priced in), but we think January is more likely.
The widening rate diﬀerential has pushed USD/CAD close to the
October highs (1.430 intraday). That is despite the Canadian dollar
outperforming all other G10 currencies over the past week, in line

with its lower negative correlation with front-end USD swings. While

### Page 4

FX
not our base case, a break of 1.430 would technically pave the way
for much more upside, with 1.450 the next key resistance.
Francesco Pesole
CEE: Vain wait for sustainable relief
The second half of the month is typically quieter in the CEE region.
Today, Poland’s core inﬂation for October will be released; we
estimate it has fallen to 2.8%. In the Czech Republic, October PPI
ﬁgures will be published – a metric that has consistently surprised
on the upside in recent months.

Tomorrow, the National Bank of Hungary decides on interest rates;
we expect no change, with the rate remaining at 5.50%. Inﬂation
has rebounded, and we anticipate further upside. Although the
headline ﬁgure should remain near the inﬂation target, global
uncertainty will likely lead the central bank to delay rate cuts and
focus instead on maintaining a stable exchange rate. Throughout
the remainder of the week, we will see more secondary data, such

as wage ﬁgures in Hungary and industrial production in Poland.
On the global front, we have seen little change in recent days. If
anything, the situation for the CEE region has deteriorated further.
The US dollar is testing new highs, and global energy prices have
seen a rebound. Meanwhile, central banks in the region remain on
the dovish side. Overall, this leaves our view – which has been
bearish on CEE FX since the end of the summer – essentially

unchanged looking forward.
EUR/CZK has likely absorbed all the dovishness from the Czech
National Bank's last meeting. We believe the repricing is complete,
and current levels around 24.400 appear fair. On the other hand,
EUR/PLN and EUR/HUF likely have more upside. However, the forint
could receive some support from the NBH tomorrow, if our hawkish
view that the central bank will not rush into rate cuts proves correct.
Frantisek Taborsky

TRPR_82201_20261022

Document 409: OPECプラスは増産見送りを継続、2027年の生産枠設定が次の焦点 ～足元の供給不足と将来的な供給過剰の双方をにらむ難しい舵取りが続く～

Source PDF: all report pdf/OPECプラスは増産見送りを継続、2027年の生産枠設定が次の焦点 ～足元の供給不足と将来的な供給過剰の双方をにらむ難しい舵取りが続く～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 4
Status: ok

### Page 1

World Trends

2026 年 11 月 2 日

1/4 
OPEC プラスは増産見送りを継続、2027 年の生産枠設定が次の焦点 
～足元の供給不足と将来的な供給過剰の双方をにらむ難しい舵取りが続く～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ OPEC プラス有志７ヵ国は 11 月 1 日のオンライン会合で、12 月の原油生産目標を据え置き、
３ヵ月連続で増産を見送った。７ヵ国合計の生産目標は日量約 3,101 万バレルである。 
○ 据え置きの背景には、イラン情勢の緊迫化がある。ホルムズ海峡などで原油輸送に支障が生
じており、目標を引き上げても実際の供給増にはつながりにくい。ＩＥＡによれば、９月の
湾岸産油国の原油輸出量は日量 1,390 万バレルと、前月から増えたものの緊迫化前の約４分
の３にとどまる。石油製品やＬＰＧの輸出は戦前の半分以下にとどまるほか、10 月には中東
のエネルギー関連施設や船舶への攻撃も再び強まった。 
○ 供給制約により原油価格は高水準で推移し、運賃や保険料の上昇も調達コストを押し上げて
いる。一方、高値の長期化は世界経済を下押しし、需要減退を招く懸念がある。ＩＥＡは 2026
年の世界石油需要を前年比で日量260 万バレル減と見込んでいる。 
○ 今後の焦点は、11 月 29 日に予定される OPEC プラス閣僚会合での 2027 年の生産枠の協議と
なる。日量約 200 万バレルの協調減産の扱いや、新たな生産基準の設定が論点だが、イラン
情勢が評価作業を複雑にしている。ＩＥＡは 2027 年に供給が日量 770 万バレル増える一方、
需要の回復は同 200 万バレルにとどまると予想しており、供給回復後には需給が緩む可能性
がある。ただし、OPEC とＩＥＡの需要見通しには隔たりがあり、先行きは不透明である。 
○ 日本など輸入国にとっても、原油高の長期化は貿易収支の悪化やインフレ圧力の増幅につな
がる。当面は、OPEC プラスの会合内容、ホルムズ海峡の航行状況、湾岸産油国の生産・輸出
動向、世界の石油需要を注視する必要がある。

【OPECプラス有志７ヵ国は 12 月の生産目標を据え置き、３ヵ月連続で増産を見送る決定】 
OPEC（石油輸出国機構）にロシアなどの主要産油国を加えた OPEC プラスの有志７ヵ国（サウジ
アラビア、ロシア、イラク、クウェート、カザフスタン、アルジェリア、オマーン）は、11 月１日
にオンライン形式で会合を開催し、12 月の原油生産目標を 11 月から据え置くことを決定した。こ
れにより、３ヵ月連続で増産が見送られることとなった。７ヵ国合計の生産目標は日量約3,101 万

### Page 2

2/4 
バレルとなり、９月以来の水準を維持する。今回の声明では、世界の原油市場の動向や先行きを検
討したうえで生産目標の据え置きを決定したと説明するとともに、各国が協調減産の枠組みを順守
する姿勢をあらためて確認した。

【イラン情勢の緊迫化に伴う供給制約を背景に、生産目標と実際の生産量の乖離が続いている】 
OPEC プラス有志国は、2023 年４月に発表し、翌５月から実施した日量 165 万バレルの自主減産に
ついて、段階的な縮小を進めてきた。2026 年９月には、この自主減産の縮小を完了したものの、そ
の後は 10 月、11 月と生産目標を据え置いており、今回も同様の判断を下した。背景には、イラン
情勢の緊迫化を受けて中東地域からの
原油輸送に支障が生じており、各国が生
産目標を引き上げても、実際の供給拡大
につながりにくい状況が続いているこ
とがある（図１）。特に、世界有数の原油
輸送の要衝であるホルムズ海峡では、船
舶の航行を巡る安全上の懸念が残り、原
油輸送に制約が生じている。湾岸産油国
は代替輸送ルートの活用などによって
輸出の回復を図っているものの、多くの
国の原油生産量は依然として生産目標
を下回っている。こうした状況では、生産目標を引き上げても実際の供給拡大につながる効果は限
定的であり、有志国は現行の生産目標を維持することが妥当と判断したと考えられる。 
実際、ＩＥＡ（国際エネルギー機関）が公表した最新の石油市場月報によれば、９月の湾岸産油
国の原油輸出量は日量1,390 万バレルと、前月から同 440 万バレル増加したものの、イラン情勢が
緊迫化する以前の水準の４分の３程度にとどまる。さらに、原油輸出は回復する一方、石油製品や
ＬＰＧ（液化石油ガス）の輸出量は依然として戦前の半分以下にとどまり、供給制約の解消には至
っていない。10 月には中東地域のエネルギー関連施設や船舶を対象とする攻撃が再び強まり、輸送
を巡る不確実性も高まっている。このため、OPEC プラスが生産目標を引き上げても、それが実際の
原油供給量の増加に結び付くとは限らず、足元の供給不足を解消するうえでは、イラン情勢の安定
化と原油輸送の正常化が不可欠といえる。

3/4 
【原油市場では供給制約が価格を下支えする一方、高値の長期化による需要減退も懸念】 
こうした供給制約は、国際原油市況を
押し上げている（図２）。足元の原油価格
は、イラン情勢が緊迫化する以前の水準
を大きく上回るなど、原油市場では供給
不足への警戒が根強く残っている。さら
に、原油価格の高止まりに加えて海上輸
送に伴う運賃や保険料なども上昇してお
り、原油や石油製品の調達コストを押し
上げる要因となっている。こうした状況
の下では、仮に OPEC プラスが増産に踏み
切ったとしても、供給制約が残る限り原
油価格の大幅な下落にはつながりにくいと考えられる。 
一方、原油価格の高止まりが世界経済の下押し圧力となることも懸念される。原油高に伴うエネ
ルギー価格の上昇は、家計の実質購買力を低下させるとともに、企業の生産・輸送コストを押し上
げることで、個人消費や設備投資などの重しとなる。IEA は、最新の石油市場月報において、2026
年の世界の石油需要が前年比で日量 260 万バレル減少するとの見通しを示しており、供給制約に伴
う石油製品の供給不足や価格高騰などが需要を下押しすると見込んでいる。このように需要の減退
が進めば、将来的には原油需給が緩和する可能性もある。そのため、OPEC プラスにとっては、足元
の供給制約への対応だけでなく、原油高が世界経済や石油需要に及ぼす影響にも配慮した政策運営
が求められる。

【今後の焦点は2027 年の生産枠設定、供給回復に伴う需給緩和の可能性も課題】 
先行きについては、OPEC プラスが11 月29 日に予定する次回会合での協議が注目される。今回の
有志７ヵ国による会合とは異なり、次回は OPEC プラス全体の閣僚会合が予定されており、2027 年
の生産枠を巡る協議が主要な焦点になるとみられる。OPEC プラスは、2023 年４月の自主減産につ
いては９月に縮小を完了したものの、別途実施している日量約 200 万バレルの協調減産措置は 2026
年末まで維持している。2027 年以降の政策運営を巡っては、この減産措置の取り扱いが重要な論点
となる。さらに、加盟各国の持続可能な最大生産能力に関する評価を踏まえ、新たな生産基準を設
定する方針であるものの、イラン情勢の悪化に伴う生産設備や生産能力の拡張計画への影響などが
評価作業を複雑化させている。 
その意味では、今後の OPEC プラスの政策運営は一段と難しさを増すと予想される。イラン情勢
の緊迫化が続き、原油輸送の制約が長期化すれば、世界的な供給不足が続くことで原油価格には上
昇圧力が掛かりやすい。一方で、停戦協議の進展などをきっかけに原油輸送が正常化すれば、これ
まで供給が制約されてきた原油が市場に流入することにより、需給環境が一転して緩和する可能性
がある。ＩＥＡは2027 年の世界の石油供給量が前年比で日量770 万バレル増加すると見込む一方、

需要の回復幅は同 200 万バレルにとどまると予想しており、供給回復の進展に伴う需給緩和の可能
性を示唆している。もっとも、OPEC（日量１億 830 万バレル）とＩＥＡ（同１億 440 万バレル）の
需要見通しの間には大きな隔たりがあり、需給の先行きには不確実性が残る。したがって、OPEC プ
ラスにとっては、供給不足が続く現状と、将来的な供給過剰の双方をにらみながら、加盟国間の利
害を調整していく必要がある。
日本をはじめとする原油輸入国にとっても、原油需給を巡る不確実性は景気や物価の先行きを左
右する重要な要因となる。原油価格の高止まりは、輸入額の増加を通じて貿易収支に下押し圧力を
もたらすほか、エネルギー価格や物流コストの上昇を通じてインフレ圧力を増幅させる懸念がある。

一方、原油輸送の正常化によって供給量が回復すれば、原油価格の下落を通じてインフレ圧力が和
らぐことも期待される。ただし、当面は中東情勢を巡る不透明感が根強く、原油価格は地政学的な
動向に左右されやすい状況が続くと考えられる。今後は、次回の OPEC プラス閣僚会合での協議内
容に加え、ホルムズ海峡の航行状況、湾岸産油国の実際の生産・輸出動向、世界の石油需要の変化
などを注意深く見極める必要がある。

September data shows that Turkey's large current account
surplus is attributable to a moderation in domestic demand
and lower net gold imports. Its capital account, on the other
hand, saw outﬂows amid volatility in investment funds
Muhammet Mercan
Turkey's surplus widened in September to US$2.7bn, almost in line
with consensus. This is versus a mere $0.6bn in the same month
last year, narrowing the 12-month rolling current account deﬁcit to
$39.2bn – approximately 2.4% of GDP – from $41.3bn a month

ago.
A closer look at the monthly ﬁgures shows that the growing surplus
was primarily due to a lower trade gap, which improved from
-$5.4bn to -$3.6bn on the back of a) an increase in core trade
surplus and b) gold trade turning positive, despite a deterioration in
energy trade balance as the US-Iran war has pushed energy prices
up. Higher services income, more than oﬀsetting deterioration in

Breakdown of the current account (US$bn)
Source: CBT, ING
In the ﬁrst nine months of 2026, Turkey's current account deﬁcit
widened by $8.9bn compared with the same period in 2025,
reaching $28.0bn. The deterioration was driven mainly by a
$12.4bn narrowing of the core trade surplus and a $1.9bn widening
of the energy deﬁcit, partly oﬀset by a $3.0bn narrowing of the

gold deﬁcit and a $3.1bn higher services income.
On the capital account side, outﬂows continued at $13.7bn in
September, given the impact of investment fund volatility following
the weakness in August. With slight inﬂows from net errors and
omissions of $1.0bn, and considering the current account balance,
oﬃcial reserves depleted by $10.0bn.
Further analysis reveals that resident activity led to outﬂows
totalling $7.0bn, primarily from continuing acquisition of portfolio

assets and higher deposits abroad. Non-residents reduced their
Turkey exposure by cutting their investments in funds by $5.8bn
following the local fund shock and deposits held at local banks by
$1.1bn.

increase in September. In the breakdown of net borrowing,
